\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})\otimes_{A_{v}}L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;{\hat{M}}\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\,,

(3.2)

which is
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
and
\tau
-equivariant, where on the left module
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
acts on both factors and
\tau
is
{\rm id}\otimes\hat{\sigma}
and on the right module
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
acts only on
L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and
\tau
is
(\tau_{\hat{M}}\circ\hat{\sigma}^{*}_{\hat{M}})\otimes\hat{\sigma}
. Since
(L^{\rm sep})^{{\mathscr{G}}_{L}}=L
we obtain

{\hat{M}}\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\;=\;(\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})\otimes_{A_{v}}L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})^{{\mathscr{G}}_{L}}.

It turns out, see
[
HK15
, Remark 4.3]
, that the isomorphism (
3.2
) extends to an equivariant isomorphism

h\colon\,\textstyle\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})\otimes_{A_{v}}L^{\rm sep}{\langle{\frac{z}{\zeta}}\rangle}\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;{\hat{M}}\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L^{\rm sep}\langle\frac{z}{\zeta}\rangle\,,

(3.3)

where for an
r\in{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
we use the notation

\displaystyle L^{\rm sep}\langle\tfrac{z}{\zeta^{r}}\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\Bigl\{\,\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}b_{i}z^{i}\colon\;b_{i}\in L^{\rm sep},\,|b_{i}|\,|\zeta|^{ri}\to 0\;(i\to+\infty)\,\Bigr\}\,.

These are subrings of
L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and the endomorphism
\hat{\sigma}\colon\sum_{i}b_{i}z^{i}\mapsto\sum_{i}b_{i}^{q_{v}}z^{i}
of
L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
restricts to a homomorphism
\hat{\sigma}\colon L^{\rm sep}\langle\tfrac{z}{\zeta^{r}}\rangle\to L^{\rm sep}\langle\tfrac{z}{\zeta^{rq_{v}}}\rangle
. Note that the
\tau
-equivariance of
h
means
h\otimes{\rm id}_{L^{\rm sep}\langle\frac{z}{\zeta^{q_{v}}}\rangle}=\tau_{\hat{M}}\circ\hat{\sigma}^{*}h
. Now the period isomorphism is defined as

\displaystyle h_{v,{\rm dR}}:=(\tau_{\hat{M}}^{-1}\circ h)\otimes{\rm id}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}}\colon\;\Koh^{1}_{v}\bigl({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}^{*}\hat{M}\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}

(3.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}\bigl({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\bigr)\,.

4
Local Shtukas with Complex Multiplication

Definition 4.1
.

Let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
be a local
\hat{\sigma}
-shtuka over
R
and assume that there is a commutative, semi-simple
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}\subset\QEnd_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}):=\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})\otimes_{A_{v}}Q_{v}
with
\dim_{Q_{v}}E_{v}=\rk{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
. Then we say that
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
has
complex multiplication
(
by
E_{v}
).

Here again semi-simple means that
E_{v}
is a direct product
E_{v}=E_{v,1}\times\cdots\times E_{v,s}
of finite field extensions of
Q_{v}
. We do
not
assume that
E_{v}
is itself a field and in Section
4
we do
not
assume that the
E_{v,i}
are separable over
Q_{v}
. We let
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
be the integral closure of
A_{v}
in
E_{v}
. It is a product
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}={\cal{O}}_{E_{v,1}}\times\cdots\times{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,s}}
of complete discrete valuation rings where
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
is the integral closure of
A_{v}
in the field
E_{v,i}
. For every
i
we write
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}={\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and set
f_{i}:=[{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}:{\mathbb{F}}_{v}]
and
e_{i}:=\ord_{y_{i}}(z)
. Then
f_{i}\,e_{i}=[E_{v,i}:Q_{v}]
and
e_{i}
is divisible by the inseparability degree of
E_{v,i}
over
Q_{v}
. Also we write
\tilde{q}_{i}:=\#{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}=q_{v}^{f_{i}}
.

Proposition 4.2
.

If
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
has complex multiplication by
E_{v}
, then there is a local shtuka
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime}
over
R
quasi-isogenous to
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
with
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\subset\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime})
.

Proof.

The
A_{v}
-submodule
T^{\prime}:={\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\cdot\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})\subset\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},Q_{v})
is
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
-invariant and contains
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})
. Since
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\subset\QEnd_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})=\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})\otimes_{A_{v}}Q_{v}
there is an element
a\in A_{v}
with
a\cdot{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\subset\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})
, and therefore
a\cdot T^{\prime}\subset\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})
is a finitely generated
A_{v}
-module, that is an
A_{v}
-lattice. By
[
HK15
, Proposition 4.22]
there is a local shtuka
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime}
and a quasi-isogeny
f\colon{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}\to{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime}
which maps
T^{\prime}
isomorphically onto
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime},A_{v})
. In particular
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
acts as
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
-equivariant endomorphisms of
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime},A_{v})
. Since the functor
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime}\mapsto\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime},A_{v})
from local shtukas to
A_{v}[{\mathscr{G}}_{L}]
-modules is fully faithful by
[
HK15
, Theorem 4.20]
, we see that
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\subset\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\prime})
.
∎

Definition 4.3
.

If
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\subset\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})
we say that
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
has
complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
. This makes the underlying module
\hat{M}
into a module over the ring
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}:={\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\otimes_{A_{v}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}={\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}{\widehat{\otimes}}_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}}R
. For
a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
note that
a\otimes 1\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}
acts on
\hat{M}
as the endomorphism
a
and on
\hat{\sigma}^{*}\hat{M}
as the endomorphism
\hat{\sigma}^{*}a
and
\tau_{\hat{M}}
is
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-linear because
a\circ\tau_{\hat{M}}=\tau_{\hat{M}}\circ\hat{\sigma}^{*}a
.

4.4
.

Let us assume that
L
contains
\psi(E_{v})
for every
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
. This implies
\psi({\cal{O}}_{E_{v}})\subset R
for every
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
. Under this assumption let us describe the ring
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}=\prod_{i=1}^{s}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i},R}
. Fix an
i
and choose and fix an
{\mathbb{F}}_{v}
-homomorphism
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}\hookrightarrow\kappa
. Then
H_{\tilde{v}_{i}}:=\Hom_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}\!}({\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}},\kappa)\cong{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i}{\mathbb{Z}}
under the map that sends
j\in{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i}{\mathbb{Z}}
to the homomorphism
(\lambda\mapsto\lambda^{q_{v}^{j}})\in H_{\tilde{v}_{i}}
. Also

{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}\otimes_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}}R\;=\;\prod_{H_{\tilde{v}_{i}}}R\;=\;\prod_{j\in{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i}{\mathbb{Z}}}{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}\otimes_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}}R\,/\,(\lambda\otimes 1-1\otimes\lambda^{q_{v}^{j}}:\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}})

and
\hat{\sigma}^{*}
transports the
j
-th factor to the
(j+1)
-th factor. Denote by
{\mathfrak{b}}_{i,j}\subset{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
the ideal generated by
(\lambda\otimes 1-1\otimes\lambda^{q_{v}^{j}}:\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}})
. Then

{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i},R}\;:=\;{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\otimes_{A_{v}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\;=\;\prod_{j\in{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i}{\mathbb{Z}}}{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\otimes_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}/{\mathfrak{b}}_{i,j}\;=\;\prod_{H_{\tilde{v}_{i}}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}.

(4.1)

Definition 4.5
.

For every
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
we let
i(\psi)
be such that
\psi
factors through the quotient
E_{v}\twoheadrightarrow E_{v,i(\psi)}
and we let
j(\psi)\in{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i(\psi)}{\mathbb{Z}}
be the element such that
\psi(\lambda)=\lambda^{q_{v}^{j(\psi)}}
for all
\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i(\psi)}}
. Then the morphism
\psi\colon{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\to R
equals the composition
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\hookrightarrow{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}\twoheadrightarrow{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)},R}/\bigl({\mathfrak{b}}_{i(\psi),j(\psi)},\,y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)
and
H_{E_{v,i}}=\{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}\colon i(\psi)=i\}
.

Lemma 4.6
.

Let
p^{m}
be the inseparability degree of
E_{v,i}
over
Q_{v}
. Then in the
j
-th component
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
of (
4.1
) we have

z-\zeta\;=\;\varepsilon\cdot\prod_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}\colon(i,j)(\psi)=(i,j)}\bigl(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})\bigr)^{p^{m}}

(4.2)

for a unit
\varepsilon\in R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
.

Proof.

Set
y^{\prime}_{i}:=y_{i}^{p^{m}}
and let
P=P(z,Y)=\sum_{\mu,\nu}b_{\mu\nu}z^{\mu}Y^{\nu}\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[Y]
with
b_{\mu\nu}\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}
be the minimal polynomial of
y^{\prime}_{i}
over
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
. It is an Eisenstein polynomial of degree
e_{i}/p^{m}
, because
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}y^{\prime}_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
is purely ramified and separable over
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
by Lemma
A.2
in the appendix. In particular
b_{0,\nu}=0
for
0\leq\nu<e_{i}/p^{m}
, and
b_{1,0}\neq 0
. Consider the polynomials
P^{(j)}(z,Y):=\sum_{\mu,\nu}b_{\mu\nu}^{q_{v}^{j}}z^{\mu}Y^{\nu}\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[Y]\subset R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[Y]
and
P^{(j)}(\zeta,Y)\in R[Y]
. If
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
satisfies
(i,j)(\psi)=(i,j)
then
P^{(j)}\bigl(\zeta,\psi(y^{\prime}_{i})\bigr)=\psi\bigl(P(z,y^{\prime}_{i})\bigr)=\psi(0)=0
. These zeroes
\psi(y^{\prime}_{i})
of
P^{(j)}(\zeta,Y)
in
L
are pairwise different, because if
\psi(y^{\prime}_{i})={\widetilde{\psi}}(y^{\prime}_{i})
then
(i,j)(\psi)=(i,j)({\widetilde{\psi}})
implies that
\psi
and
{\widetilde{\psi}}
coincide on
E_{v,i}
and hence on
E_{v}
. It follows that
P^{(j)}(\zeta,Y)=\prod_{\psi\colon(i,j)(\psi)=(i,j)}(Y-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}))
in
L[Y]
, whence already in
R[Y]
. In the
j
-th component
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
of (
4.1
) we have
0=\sum_{\mu,\nu}b_{\mu\nu}z^{\mu}(y^{\prime}_{i})^{\nu}\otimes 1=\sum_{\mu,\nu}(y^{\prime}_{i})^{\nu}\otimes b_{\mu\nu}^{q_{v}^{j}}z^{\mu}=P^{(j)}(z,y^{\prime}_{i})
, and we compute

\displaystyle\prod_{\psi\colon(i,j)(\psi)=(i,j)}\bigl(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})\bigr)^{p^{m}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P^{(j)}(\zeta,y^{\prime}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P^{(j)}(\zeta,y^{\prime}_{i})-P^{(j)}(z,y^{\prime}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu,\nu}}b_{\mu\nu}^{q_{v}^{j}}(\zeta^{\mu}-z^{\mu})(y^{\prime}_{i})^{\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\zeta-z)\cdot{\textstyle\sum\limits_{\mu,\nu}}b_{\mu\nu}^{q_{v}^{j}}(\zeta^{\mu-1}+\zeta^{\mu-2}z+\ldots+z^{\mu-1})(y^{\prime}_{i})^{\nu}\,.

The factor
\sum_{\mu,\nu}b_{\mu\nu}^{q_{v}^{j}}(\zeta^{\mu-1}+\zeta^{\mu-2}z+\ldots+z^{\mu-1})(y^{\prime}_{i})^{\nu}
is congruent to
b_{1,0}^{q_{v}^{j}}\neq 0
modulo the maximal ideal
(\pi_{L},y_{i})\subset R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and therefore a unit in
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. This finishes the proof. Note that since

P^{(j)}(\zeta,y^{\prime}_{i})-P^{(j)}(z,y^{\prime}_{i})\;=\;{\textstyle\sum\limits_{n\geq 1}}\tfrac{1}{n!}\,\tfrac{\partial^{n}P^{(j)}}{\partial z^{n}}(z,y^{\prime}_{i})\cdot(\zeta-z)^{n}\,,

the proof could also be phrased by saying that
\tfrac{\partial P^{(j)}}{\partial z}(z,y^{\prime}_{i})=\sum_{\mu,\nu}\mu b_{\mu\nu}^{q_{v}^{j}}z^{\mu-1}(y^{\prime}_{i})^{\nu}
lies in
{\cal{O}}_{E^{\prime}_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. In fact this partial derivative is congruent to
b_{1,0}^{q_{v}^{j}}\neq 0
modulo
y^{\prime}_{i}\cdot{\cal{O}}_{E^{\prime}_{v,i}}
.
∎

Let us draw a direct corollary from the proof of this lemma. To formulate it, recall that if
E_{v,i}
is separable over
Q_{v}
, the different
{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}
of
E_{v,i}
over
Q_{v}
is defined as the ideal in
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
which annihilates the module
\Omega^{1}_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}/A_{v}}
of relative differentials.

Corollary 4.7
.

If
E_{v,i}
is separable over
Q_{v}
then
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\varphi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}}=\Bigl(\tfrac{z-\zeta}{y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})}\Big|_{y_{i}=\varphi(y_{i})}\Bigr)
in
{\cal{O}}_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}
for every
\varphi\in H_{E_{v,i}}
.

Proof.

By
[
Ser79
, Chapter III, § 4, Proposition 8]
the different is multiplicative, that is
{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}={\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i}/{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}}\cdot{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}/Q_{v}}
. Moreover,
{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}/Q_{v}}=1
because
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
is unramified over
A_{v}
. As in the proof of the preceding lemma let
P(z,Y)
be the minimal polynomial of
y^{\prime}_{i}
over
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
and note that
y^{\prime}_{i}=y_{i}
under our separability assumption. Then
\tfrac{\partial P}{\partial z}(z,y_{i})\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
and

\Omega^{1}_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}/{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}\;=\;{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\langle dz,dy_{i}\rangle\big/\bigl(dz,\,\tfrac{\partial P}{\partial z}(z,y_{i})dz+\tfrac{\partial P}{\partial Y}(z,y_{i})dy_{i}\bigr)\;=\;{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\cdot dy_{i}/(\tfrac{\partial P}{\partial Y}(z,y_{i})dy_{i})\,.

We write
z=f(y_{i})\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. Then
0=\tfrac{d}{dy_{i}}P(f(y_{i}),y_{i})=\tfrac{\partial P}{\partial z}(f(y_{i}),y_{i})\tfrac{df(y_{i})}{dy_{i}}+\tfrac{\partial P}{\partial Y}(f(y_{i}),y_{i})
and
{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}=\bigl(\tfrac{\partial P}{\partial Y}(z,y_{i})\bigr)=\bigl(\tfrac{df(y_{i})}{dy_{i}}\bigr)
. Now Lemma
A.1
in the appendix implies that
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\varphi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}}=\varphi\bigl(\tfrac{df(y_{i})}{dy_{i}}\bigr)=\Bigl(\tfrac{z-\zeta}{y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})}\Bigr)\Big|_{y_{i}=\varphi(y_{i})}
.
∎

Now we explore the consequences of these decompositions for local shtukas with complex multiplication.

Proposition 4.8
.

Let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}=(\hat{M},\tau_{\hat{M}})
have complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
. Then the
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}
-module
\hat{M}
is free of rank
1
. In particular,
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i}:={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
is a local
\hat{\sigma}
-shtuka over
R
with
\rk{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i}=[E_{v,i}:Q_{v}]
and
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}=\bigoplus_{i=1}^{s}{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i}
.

Proof.

By faithfully flat descent
[
EGA
, IV
2
, Proposition 2.5.2]
, we may replace
R
by a finite extension of discrete valuation rings. Therefore it suffices to prove the proposition in the case where
R
contains
\psi({\cal{O}}_{E_{v}})
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
. In this case
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}
is a product of two dimensional regular local rings
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
by (
4.1
). By
[
Ser58
, §6, Lemme 6]
a finitely generated module
M
over such a ring is free if and only if it is reflexive, that is
M
is isomorphic to its bidual
M^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}}
, where
M^{{}^{\vee}}=\Hom_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}(M,R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. In particular
M^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}}
, which is isomorphic to
(M^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}})^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}}
is free. We apply this to
M:=\hat{M}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and consider the base changes
M\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}=M\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and
M\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{y_{i}}]=M\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{z}]
. Like
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
also
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{y_{i}}]
is a principal ideal domain, because it is a factorial ring of dimension
1
. Using
[
Eis95
, Proposition 2.10]
and that both base changes of
M
are torsion free, whence free, we see that the canonical morphism
M\to M^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}}
is an isomorphism after both base changes. Since
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}=L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\cap R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{z}]\subset L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
and
M
and
M^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}}
are free
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-modules,
M
equals the intersection
(M\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\cap(M\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{z}])
inside
M\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
, and likewise for
M^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}}
. This shows that
M\to M^{{}^{\vee}}{}^{{}^{\vee}}
is an isomorphism and
M
is free over
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
.

It remains to compute the rank. Let
r_{i,j}:=\rk_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}(\hat{M}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i},R}/{\mathfrak{b}}_{i,j})
for all
i=1,\ldots,s
and all
j\in{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i}{\mathbb{Z}}
. Then
\sum_{i,j}r_{i,j}\cdot e_{i}=\rk{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
. We first prove that for a fixed
i
all
r_{i,j}
are equal. Since
(\hat{\sigma}^{*}\hat{M})\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i},R}/{\mathfrak{b}}_{i,j}=\hat{\sigma}^{*}(\hat{M}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i},R}/{\mathfrak{b}}_{i,j-1})\cong R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{r_{i,j-1}}
, we can write the isomorphism
\tau_{\hat{M}}:\hat{\sigma}^{*}\hat{M}[\frac{1}{z-\zeta}]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\hat{M}[\frac{1}{z-\zeta}]
in the form

\prod_{j}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{z-\zeta}]^{r_{i,j-1}}\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;\prod_{j}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{z-\zeta}]^{r_{i,j}},

which gives us
r_{i,j-1}=r_{i,j}=:r_{i}
for all
j
, and hence
\sum_{i}r_{i}\,f_{i}\,e_{i}=\rk{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}=\dim_{Q_{v}}E_{v}=\sum_{i}\dim_{Q_{v}}E_{v,i}=\sum_{i}f_{i}\,e_{i}
. Thus if we prove that
r_{i}\neq 0
then all
r_{i}
must be
1
and so
\hat{M}
is a free
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}
-module of rank
1
and
\rk{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i}=f_{i}\,e_{i}=[E_{v,i}:Q_{v}]
. Now
r_{i}=0
means that
\hat{M}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}=(0)
, and hence
E_{v,i}
acts as zero on
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
in contradiction to
E_{v}\subset\QEnd_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Proposition 4.9
.

If
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
has complex multiplication by a commutative semi-simple
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}
then
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},Q_{v})
is a free
E_{v}
-module of rank
1
and
\Koh^{1}_{{\rm dR}}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
is a free
E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-module of rank one, where the homomorphism
Q_{v}={\mathbb{F}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\to L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
is given by
z\mapsto z=\zeta+(z-\zeta)
. If we assume that
L\supset\psi(E_{v})
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
then the decomposition (
A.1
) induces a decomposition

\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\;=\;\bigoplus_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}}\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}),

(4.3)

where
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
is free of rank
1
over
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. In particular,

\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)\;=\;\bigoplus_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}}\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)\otimes_{R}L

(4.4)

is the decomposition into generalized eigenspaces of the
E_{v}
-action. Here

\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)\;:=\;\bigl\{\omega\in\Koh^{1}_{{\rm dR}}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)\colon\bigl([a]^{*}-\psi(a)\bigr)^{[E_{v,i(\psi)}:Q_{v}]_{\rm insep}}\cdot\omega=0\kern 5.0pt\forall\;a\in E_{v}\cap\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})\bigr\}

is a free
R
-module of rank equal to the inseparability degree
[E_{v,i(\psi)}:Q_{v}]_{\rm insep}
of
E_{v,i(\psi)}
over
Q_{v}
.

Proof.

By the faithfulness of the functor
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}\to\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},Q_{v})
we have
E_{v}\subset\End_{Q_{v}}\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},Q_{v})
. So the first statement follows from
[
BH09
, Lemma 7.2]
.

Since
\Koh^{1}_{{\rm dR}}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
is an isogeny invariant, we may by Proposition
4.2
assume that
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\subset\End_{R}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,})
and then
\hat{M}
is free of rank
1
over
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}
by Proposition
4.8
. It follows that
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}):=\hat{\sigma}^{*}\hat{M}\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\cong E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. Now we use Lemma
A.3
. In particular, (
4.4
) and the statement about
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
follow from (
A.2
) and the equation
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)=\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)\otimes_{R}L
.
∎

The proposition allows us to make two definitions.

Definition 4.10
.

Let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
have complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
and assume that
L\supset\psi(E_{v})
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
. Fix a
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
and let
i:=i(\psi)
. Let
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\in\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
be an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator whose reduction
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\;{\rm mod}\;(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}))\in\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)\big/(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}))\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)
is a generator of the free
R
-module of rank one
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)\big/(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}))\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
. Such a
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
is uniquely determined up to multiplication by an element of
R^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}+(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}))L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. Note that if
E_{v,i}
is separable over
Q_{v}
then
y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})
acts trivially on
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)
and
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
is a free
R
-module of rank
1
. Also
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}=L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
.

If
\omega_{\psi}\in\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
is any generator, there is an element
x\in L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
with
\omega_{\psi}=x\,\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
. We define the
valuation of
\omega_{\psi}
as
v(\omega_{\psi}):=v\bigl(x\;{\rm mod}\;y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})\bigr)
.
(NOTE THAT THIS DEFINITION IS WRONG; SEE ERRATUM
B.2
)
It only depends on the image of
\omega_{\psi}
in
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)
and is also independent of the choice of
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
.

Note that if
{\underline{M\!}\,}=(M,\tau_{M})
is an
A
-motive over
L
with good model
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
over

, and

, then for an

-generator
\omega_{\psi}\in\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{M\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})=\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
the present definition of

coincides with the definition of

from (

).

Definition 4.11
.

A
local CM-type
at
v
is a pair
(E_{v},\Phi)
with
E_{v}
a semisimple commutative
Q_{v}
-algebra and
\Phi=(d_{\psi})_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}}
a tuple of integers
d_{\psi}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
.

If
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
is a local shtuka with complex multiplication by a commutative semi-simple
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}
and if
L\supset\psi(E_{v})
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
then the Hodge-Pink lattice
{\mathfrak{q}}^{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}=\tau_{\hat{M}}^{-1}(\hat{M}\otimes_{R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
satisfies
{\mathfrak{q}}^{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}=\prod_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}}\bigl(y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)^{-d_{\psi}}\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
for integers
d_{\psi}
under the decomposition (
4.3
). We call
\Phi=(d_{\psi})_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}}
the
local CM-type
of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
.

Note that if
{\underline{M\!}\,}=(M,\tau_{M})
is an
A
-motive over
L
with good model
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
over
R
, and
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{v}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}})
is the local shtuka at
v
associated with
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
as in Example
3.2
, and
E_{v}:=E\otimes_{Q}Q_{v}
, then we see from the isomorphism
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{M\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})=\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
that the local CM-type of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
is equal to the CM-type of
{\underline{M\!}\,}
under the identification
H_{E}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}H_{E_{v}}
, which extends
\psi\colon E\to Q^{\rm alg}\subset Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
to the completion
\psi\colon E_{v}\to Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
.

5
Periods of Local Shtukas with Complex Multiplication

5.1
.

In this section we let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
be a local
\hat{\sigma}
-shtuka over
R
with complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
where
E_{v}
is a commutative semi-simple
Q_{v}
-algebra as in the preceding section. From Theorem
5.13
on we assume that the factors
E_{v,i}
of
E_{v}
are
separable
field extensions of
Q_{v}
. Throughout we assume that
L\supset\psi(E_{v})
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
. Using Proposition
4.8
we may choose a basis of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
and write it under the decomposition (
4.1
) as

{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}\;\cong\;\prod_{i}\prod_{j\in{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i}{\mathbb{Z}}}(R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}},\tau_{i,j})\qquad\text{with}\quad\tau_{i,j}\in R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[\tfrac{1}{z-\zeta}]^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}\,.

Let
c\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
be such that
(z-\zeta)^{c}\tau_{ij},(z-\zeta)^{c}\tau_{ij}^{-1}\in R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. Since the
y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})
for
\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}
with
(i,j)(\varphi)=(i,j)
are prime elements in the factorial ring
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
, Lemma
4.6
applied to
(z-\zeta)^{c}\tau_{ij}\cdot(z-\zeta)^{c}\tau_{ij}^{-1}=(z-\zeta)^{2c}
shows that

\tau_{i,j}\;=\;\varepsilon_{i,j}\cdot\prod_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}\colon(i,j)(\varphi)=(i,j)}\bigl(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)^{d_{\varphi}}

(5.1)

for a unit
\varepsilon_{i,j}\in R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
and integers
d_{\varphi}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
. By Definition
4.11
the tuple
\Phi=(d_{\varphi})_{\varphi}
is the local CM-type of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
.

Note that we can view
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
as the tensor product
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0}\otimes\bigotimes_{\varphi}{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}{}^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}
over
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}
of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0}:=({\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R},\tau_{0}=\prod_{i,j}\varepsilon_{i,j})
and all the
d_{\varphi}
-th powers of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}:=({\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R},\prod_{i,j}\tau_{\varphi,i,j})
where

\tau_{\varphi,i,j}=\begin{cases}1&\text{if }(i,j)\neq(i,j)(\varphi),\\
y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})&\text{if }(i,j)=(i,j)(\varphi).\end{cases}

Likewise the cohomology realizations decompose as tensor products

\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})\;\cong\;\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},A_{v})\otimes\bigotimes_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}\,,

where the tensor product is over
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
, and

\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\;\cong\;\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\otimes\bigotimes\limits_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}\,,

where the tensor product is over
E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. For the purpose of computing the period isomorphism
h_{v,{\rm dR}}
, we may therefore treat all factors of
\tau_{i,j}
separately; see
5.4
below.

5.2
.

We first treat the case of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0}=\bigl({\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R},\tau_{0}=(\varepsilon_{i,j})_{i,j}\bigr)
, where
\varepsilon_{i,j}\in R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. We compute the
\tau
-invariants
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},A_{v})
as the set of tuples
(c_{i,j})_{i,j}
with
c_{i,j}:=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}c_{i,j,n}\,y_{i}^{n}\in L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
subject to the condition

(c_{i,j})_{i,j}\;=\;\tau_{0}\circ\hat{\sigma}\bigl((c_{i,j})_{i,j}\bigr)\,,\quad\text{that is}\quad c_{i,j}\;=\;\varepsilon_{i,j}\cdot\hat{\sigma}(c_{i,j-1})\quad\text{for all $i,j$}.

The latter implies
c_{i,j}=\varepsilon_{i,j}\cdot\hat{\sigma}(\varepsilon_{i,j-1})\cdot\ldots\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{j-1}(\varepsilon_{i,1})\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{j}(c_{i,0})
and
c_{i,0}=\varepsilon_{i}\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i}}(c_{i,0})
, where we set
\varepsilon_{i}:=\varepsilon_{i,0}\cdot\hat{\sigma}(\varepsilon_{i,f_{i}-1})\cdot\ldots\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i}-1}(\varepsilon_{i,1})=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}b_{i,n}\,y_{i}^{n}\in R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. In particular
b_{i,0}\in R^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. The resulting formulas for the coefficients

c_{i,0,0}\;=\;b_{i,0}\cdot c_{i,0,0}^{\tilde{q}_{i}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad c_{i,0,n}-b_{i,0}\cdot c_{i,0,n}^{\tilde{q}_{i}}\;=\;{\textstyle\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{n}}b_{i,\ell}\cdot c_{i,0,n-\ell}^{\tilde{q}_{i}},

where
\tilde{q}_{i}=q_{v}^{f_{i}}
, lead to the formulas

c_{i,0,0}^{\tilde{q}_{i}-1}\;=\;b_{i,0}^{-1}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\frac{c_{i,0,n}}{c_{i,0,0}}-\bigl(\frac{c_{i,0,n}}{c_{i,0,0}}\bigr)^{\tilde{q}_{i}}\;=\;\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\frac{b_{i,\ell}}{b_{i,0}}\cdot\bigl(\frac{c_{i,0,n-\ell}}{c_{i,0,0}}\bigr)^{\tilde{q}_{i}}\,,

which have solutions
c_{i,0,n}\in{\cal{O}}_{L^{\rm sep}}
with
c_{i,0,0}\in{\cal{O}}_{L^{\rm sep}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. In particular, the field extension of
L
generated by the
c_{i,j,n}
is unramified. Then
(c_{i,j})_{i,j}
is an
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-basis of
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},A_{v})
. Under the period isomorphism
h_{v,{\rm dR}}
it is mapped to

(\varepsilon_{i,j}^{-1}c_{i,j})_{i,j}\;\in\;\bigl({\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\otimes_{A_{v}}{\cal{O}}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\bigr)^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}\;\subset\;E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\;=\;\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\,.

5.3
.

Next we compute the period isomorphism for the local shtuka
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
from above. For an element
0\neq\xi\in(\pi_{L})\subset R
we consider the equation

\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i}}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi})\;=\;(y_{i}-\xi)\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}\qquad\text{for}\qquad\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}\;:=\;\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\ell_{n}y_{i}^{n}\;\in\;L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\,.

(5.2)

The equation can be solved by taking
\ell_{n}\in L^{\rm sep}
with
\ell_{0}^{\tilde{q}_{i}-1}=-\xi
and
\ell_{n}^{\tilde{q}_{i}}+\xi\ell_{n}=\ell_{n-1}
. This implies that
|\ell_{n}|=|\xi|^{\tilde{q}_{i}^{-n}/(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}<1
and
\ell_{n}\in{\cal{O}}_{L^{\rm sep}}
. Note that this solution is not unique, but that every other solution
\tilde{\ell}^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}
of (
5.2
) is obtained by multiplying
\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}
by an element of
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}={\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
, because
\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i}}\Bigl(\tfrac{\tilde{\ell}^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}}{\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}}\Bigr)=\tfrac{(y_{i}-\xi)\cdot\tilde{\ell}^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}}{(y_{i}-\xi)\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}}=\tfrac{\tilde{\ell}^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}}{\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\xi}}\in L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
is invariant under
\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i}}
and hence lies in
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
.

According to the decomposition (
4.1
) the
\tau
-invariants
\check{u}\in\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
have the form
\check{u}=(\check{u}_{i,j})_{i,j}\in\prod_{i,j}L^{\rm sep}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
with
\check{u}=\tau_{\varphi}\cdot\hat{\sigma}(\check{u})
, that is

\check{u}_{i,j}\;=\;\begin{cases}\hat{\sigma}(\check{u}_{i,j-1})&\text{if }(i,j)\neq(i,j)(\varphi)\,,\\
\bigl(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)\cdot\hat{\sigma}(\check{u}_{i,j-1})&\text{if }(i,j)=(i,j)(\varphi)\,.\end{cases}

For
j,j^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{Z}}/f_{i}{\mathbb{Z}}
we denote by
(j,j^{\prime})
the representative of
j-j^{\prime}
in
\{0,\ldots,f_{i}-1\}
. This implies that
\check{u}_{(i,j)(\varphi)}=\bigl(y_{i(\varphi)}-\varphi(y_{i(\varphi)})\bigr)\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i(\varphi)}}(\check{u}_{(i,j)(\varphi)})
, and
\check{u}_{i(\varphi),j}=\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}(\check{u}_{(i,j)(\varphi)})
, as well as
\check{u}_{i,j}\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
for all
i\neq i(\varphi)
and all
j
. In particular an
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-basis of
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
is given by

\check{u}\;=\;(\check{u}_{i,j})_{i,j}\qquad\text{with}\qquad\check{u}_{i,j}\;=\;\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})^{-\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\;=\;(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})^{q_{v}^{(j,j(\varphi))}}})^{-\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}

(5.3)

where
\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}
is the Kronecker
\delta
. The comparison isomorphism
h_{v,{\rm dR}}
sends this
\check{u}
to the element

\tau_{\varphi}^{-1}\cdot\check{u}\;=\;\biggl(\Bigl(\bigl(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)^{\delta_{j,j(\varphi)}}\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})\Bigr)^{-\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\biggr)_{i,j}

(5.4)

of
E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\;=\;\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}\bigl({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\bigr)
.

5.4
.

Putting everything together we see that our
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}\cong({\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R},\prod_{i,j}\tau_{i,j})
with
\tau_{i,j}
from (
5.1
) has

\check{u}\;=\;(\check{u}_{i,j})_{i,j}\;=\;\Bigl(c_{i,j}\cdot\!\prod_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})^{-d_{\varphi}}\Bigr)_{i,j}

as an
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-basis of
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})\cong\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},A_{v})\otimes\bigotimes_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}
, where the tensor product is over
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
. Under
h_{v,{\rm dR}}
this
\check{u}
is mapped to the element

\tau_{\hat{M}}^{-1}\cdot\check{u}\;=\;\biggl(\varepsilon_{i,j}^{-1}c_{i,j}\cdot\!\prod_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}\Bigl(\bigl(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)^{\delta_{j,j(\varphi)}}\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})\Bigr)^{-d_{\varphi}}\biggr)_{i,j}

(5.5)

of
E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\;\cong\;\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}\bigl({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\bigr)\cong\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\otimes\bigotimes\limits_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}
, where the tensor product is over
E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
.

Remark 5.5
.

Note that
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
with
i=i(\varphi)
is the local
\hat{\sigma}
-shtuka associated with a Lubin-Tate formal group, and so our treatment is analogous to Colmez’s
[
Col93
, § I.2]
. Namely, let
\hat{G}=\hat{\mathbb{G}}_{a,R}=\Spf R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}X{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
be the formal additive group over
R
with an action of
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}={\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
given by

\displaystyle[\lambda]:X

\displaystyle\longmapsto

\displaystyle\varphi(\lambda)\cdot X\;=\;\lambda^{q_{v}^{j(\varphi)}}\!\cdot X\qquad\text{for }\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}\,,

\displaystyle\,[y_{i}]:X

\displaystyle\longmapsto

\displaystyle X^{\tilde{q}_{i}}+\varphi(y_{i})\cdot X\,.

Then
\hat{G}
is the Lubin-Tate formal group over
R
associated with
{\cal{O}}_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}
; see
[
LT65
]
. It is a
z
-divisible local Anderson module
in the sense of
[
HS15
, Definition 7.1]
. For an element
a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
let
\hat{G}[a]:=\ker[a]
. Under the anti-equivalence between
z
-divisible local Anderson modules and effective local
\hat{\sigma}
-shtukas over
S=\Spec R
from
[
HS15
, Theorem 8.3]
the associated local shtuka is

\hat{M}\;:=\;\hat{M}(\hat{G})\;:=\;\displaystyle\lim_{\underset{n}{\longleftarrow}}\,\Hom_{R}(\hat{G}[z^{n}],{\mathbb{G}}_{a,R})\;=\;\displaystyle\lim_{\underset{n}{\longleftarrow}}\,\Hom_{R}(\hat{G}[y_{i}^{ne_{i}}],{\mathbb{G}}_{a,R})\;=\;\bigoplus_{k=0}^{f_{i}-1}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\tau^{k}

with
\tau^{0}:={\rm id}\colon\hat{G}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\hat{\mathbb{G}}_{a,R}
and
\tau^{k}:=\Frob_{q_{v},\hat{\mathbb{G}}_{a,R}}\circ\tau^{0}\colon X\mapsto X^{q_{v}^{k}}
. It is an
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i},R}
-module via the
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
-action on
\hat{G}[z^{n}]
and the
R
-action on
{\mathbb{G}}_{a,R}
, and is equipped with the Frobenius
\tau_{\hat{M}}\colon\hat{\sigma}^{*}\hat{M}\to\hat{M}
given by
\hat{\sigma}_{\!\hat{M}}^{*}m\mapsto\Frob_{q_{v},{\mathbb{G}}_{a,R}}\circ m
for
m\in\hat{M}
. We set
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G}):=(\hat{M},\tau_{\hat{M}})
. In particular, we see that
\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}
acts on
R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\tau^{k}
as
\lambda^{q_{v}^{k+j(\varphi)}}
and so
\hat{M}/{\mathfrak{b}}_{i,j}\hat{M}=R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\tau^{(j,j(\varphi))}
under the decomposition (
4.1
). Since
\tau_{\hat{M}}(\hat{\sigma}_{\!\hat{M}}^{*}\tau^{f_{i}-1})=\tau^{f_{i}}=[y_{i}]-\varphi(y_{i})\colon X\mapsto X^{\tilde{q}_{i}}=\bigl([y_{i}]-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)(X)
, we see that

\tau_{\hat{M}}\;=\;(\tau_{\hat{M},j})_{j}\qquad\text{with}\qquad\tau_{\hat{M},j}=\begin{cases}1&\text{if }j\neq j(\varphi)\,,\\
y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})&\text{if }j=j(\varphi)\,,\end{cases}

that is,
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G})={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
.

Moreover, if we want to also consider the other components of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
for
i\neq i(\varphi)
we take the divisible local Anderson module
\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi}:=\hat{G}\times\prod_{i\neq i(\varphi)}(E_{v,i}/{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}})_{R}
. It has local shtuka
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi})={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G})\oplus\bigoplus_{i\neq i(\varphi)}{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}\bigl((E_{v,i}/{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}})_{R}\bigr)={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
, because
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}\bigl((E_{v,i}/{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}})_{R}\bigr)=({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i},R},\tau=1)
.

5.6
.

We want to describe the Galois action of
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
on
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
. Recall from
[
HK15
, Definition 4.8, Proposition 4.9 and Remark 4.10]
that the Tate module of
\hat{G}
is defined as
T_{v}\hat{G}:=\Hom_{A_{v}}\bigl(Q_{v}/A_{v},\hat{G}(L^{\rm sep})\bigr)
and that there is a perfect pairing of
A_{v}
-modules

T_{v}\hat{G}\times\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G}),A_{v})\;\longrightarrow\;\Hom_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}}(Q_{v}/A_{v},{\mathbb{F}}_{v})\,,\quad(f,m)\longmapsto m\circ f\,,

(5.6)

which is equivariant for the actions of
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
and
\End_{R}\bigl({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G})\bigr)=\End_{R}(\hat{G})^{\rm op}
. Here the
A_{v}
-module
\Hom_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}}(Q_{v}/A_{v},{\mathbb{F}}_{v})\cong{\widehat{\Omega}}^{1}_{A_{v}/{\mathbb{F}}_{v}}\cong{\mathbb{F}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}dz
is free of rank one; see
[
HK15
, Equation (4.5) before Proposition 4.9]
. We have already computed
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})=E_{v,i}\cdot(\check{u}_{i,j})_{i,j}
in (
5.3
). We will now compute
T_{v}\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi}
and the action of
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
on both
T_{v}\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi}
and
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
. Let again
i=i(\varphi)
. Since
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
acts on
\hat{G}(L^{\rm sep})
we have

\displaystyle T_{v}\hat{G}\kern 5.0pt=\kern 5.0pt

\displaystyle\Hom_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}}\bigl({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\otimes_{A_{v}}(Q_{v}/A_{v}),\hat{G}(L^{\rm sep})\bigr)

\displaystyle\kern 5.0pt=\kern 5.0pt

\displaystyle\Hom_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}}\bigl(E_{v,i}/{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}},\hat{G}(L^{\rm sep})\bigr)

\displaystyle\ni f

\displaystyle\kern 5.0pt=\kern 5.0pt

\displaystyle\textstyle\bigl\{(P_{n})_{n}\in\prod\limits_{{n}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}}\hat{G}[y_{i}^{n}](L^{\rm sep})\colon[y_{i}](P_{n})=P_{n-1}\bigr\}

\displaystyle\ni(P_{n})_{n}:=\bigl(f(y_{i}^{-n})\bigr)_{n}\,,

where
f
is reconstructed from
(P_{n})_{n}
as
f(ay_{i}^{-n}):=[a](P_{n})
for
a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. From equation (
5.2
) we see that
\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\ell_{n}y_{i}^{n}
satisfies

[y_{i}](\ell_{0})\;=\;\ell_{0}^{\tilde{q}_{i}}+\varphi(y_{i})\ell_{0}\;=\;0\qquad\text{and}\qquad[y_{i}](\ell_{n})\;=\;\ell_{n}^{\tilde{q}_{i}}+\varphi(y_{i})\ell_{n}\;=\;\ell_{n-1}\,.

Thus
\ell_{n-1}\in\hat{G}[y_{i}^{n}](L^{\rm sep})
and
T_{v}\hat{G}={\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\cdot(\ell_{n-1})_{n}
. To compute the
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
-action on
T_{v}\hat{G}
we need the following

Proposition 5.7
.

Let
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}_{\infty}:={\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}(\ell_{n}:n\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0})
. Then there is an isomorphism of topological groups
