\chi\colon\Gal\bigl({\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}_{\infty}/{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\bigr)\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}\;=\;{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}

satisfying
g(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}):=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}g(\ell_{n})y_{i}^{n}=\chi(g)\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}
in
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}_{\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
for
g
in the Galois group. The isomorphism
\chi
is independent of the choice of
\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}
and is called the
cyclotomic character
of the field
E_{v,i}={\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
.

Proof.

The existence of
\chi
follows from the equation
\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i}}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})=(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}))\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}
, which implies that
\chi(g):=\frac{g(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})}{\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}}
is
\hat{\sigma}^{f_{i}}
-invariant, that is
\chi(g)\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. Furthermore,
\chi
is an isomorphism because
\ell_{n-1}
is a uniformizing parameter of
{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}(\ell_{0},\ldots,\ell_{n-1})
and so the equations defining the
\ell_{n}
are irreducible by Eisenstein. Every other solution of (
5.2
) is of the form
a\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}
with
a\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and so
g(a\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})=a\cdot g(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})=\chi(g)\cdot a\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}
. This shows that
\chi(g)
does not depend on the solution
\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}
.
∎

Let
{\cal{I}}_{L}\subset{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
be the inertia subgroup and similarly for other fields. By local class field theory, see Lubin and Tate
[
LT65
, Corollary on p. 386]
, the image of
g\in{\cal{I}}_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}
in
{\mathscr{G}}_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}^{\rm ab}
equals the norm residue symbol
\bigl(\chi(g)^{-1}|_{y_{i}=\varphi(y_{i})},\varphi(E_{v,i})^{\rm ab}/\varphi(E_{v,i})\bigr)
where
\varphi(E_{v,i})^{\rm ab}
is the maximal abelian extension of
\varphi(E_{v,i})
in
Q_{v}^{\rm sep}
. In general, the homomorphism
\chi_{L}\colon{\cal{I}}_{L}\to{\cal{O}}_{L}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
with
g|_{L^{\rm ab}}=(\chi_{L}(g)^{-1},L^{\rm ab}/L)
is sometimes called the
character of local class field theory
of the field
L
. So we see that
\chi(g)|_{y_{i}=\varphi(y_{i})}=\chi_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}(g)
. If
L
is separable over
\varphi(E_{v,i})
these characters are compatible for
g\in{\cal{I}}_{L}
in the sense that
\chi_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}(g)=N_{L/\varphi(E_{v,i})}(\chi_{L}(g))
.

5.8
.

From
T_{v}\hat{G}={\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\cdot(\ell_{n-1})_{n}
it follows that
g
acts on
T_{v}\hat{G}
in the same way as an endomorphism in
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
. Let us compute this endomorphism. We write
\chi(g)=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}a_{k}y_{i}^{k}
with
a_{k}\in{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}
. Then the expansion
g(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})=\chi(g)\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k}\ell_{n-k}y_{i}^{n}
implies that
g(\ell_{n})=\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k}\ell_{n-k}=\sum_{k=0}^{n}\varphi(a_{k}^{q_{v}^{-j(\varphi)}})[y_{i}^{k}](\ell_{n})
. Thus every element
g\in{\cal{I}}_{L}
acts on
T_{v}\hat{G}
as the endomorphism
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}a_{k}^{q_{v}^{-j(\varphi)}}y_{i}^{k}=\hat{\sigma}^{-j(\varphi)}(\chi(g))=\varphi^{-1}\bigl(\chi(g)|_{y_{i}=\varphi(y_{i})}\bigr)=\varphi^{-1}\circ\chi_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}(g)\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
and on
T_{v}\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi}
as the endomorphism
\bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{-j(\varphi)}(\chi(g))^{\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\bigr)_{i}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
.

Definition 5.9
.

We define the character
\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}:=\bigl((\varphi^{-1}\circ\chi_{\varphi(E_{v,i})})^{\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\bigr)_{i}\colon{\cal{I}}_{L}\to{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
by mapping
g\mapsto\bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{-j(\varphi)}(\chi(g))^{\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\bigr)_{i}=\bigl(1,\ldots,1,\varphi^{-1}\circ\chi_{\varphi(E_{v,i})}(g),1,\ldots,1\bigr)
.

5.10
.

Due to the equivariance of the pairing (
5.6
) under
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
and
\End_{R}(\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi})
the action of
g\in{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
on
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi}),A_{v})
is given by the endomorphism
\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g)^{-1}
. We can also compute this action directly as follows. It factors through the restriction of
g
to
\Gal\bigl({\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}_{\infty}/{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\bigr)
which we denote again by
g
. Then on the basis
(\check{u}_{i,j})_{j}
of
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G}),A_{v})
from (
5.3
) we compute

\displaystyle g(\check{u}_{i,j})_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g\Bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})^{-\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\Bigr)_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}\bigl(\chi(g)\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}\bigr)^{-\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\Bigr)_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{j-j(\varphi)}\bigl(\chi(g)^{-1}\bigr)^{\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{(j,j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})^{-\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\Bigr)_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\hat{\sigma}^{-j(\varphi)}(\chi(g)^{-1})\otimes 1)^{\delta_{i,i(\varphi)}}\cdot(\check{u}_{i,j})_{j}

for the element
\hat{\sigma}^{-j(\varphi)}(\chi(g)^{-1})\otimes 1\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\otimes_{A_{v}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. That is, the action of
g\in{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
on
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G}),A_{v})
coincides with the endomorphism
\hat{\sigma}^{-j(\varphi)}(\chi(g)^{-1})
and the action of
g\in{\cal{I}}_{L}
on
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}(\hat{G}_{E_{v},\varphi}),A_{v})=\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
coincides with the endomorphism
\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g)^{-1}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
.

Proposition 5.11
.

Let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
have complex multiplication by a commutative, semi-simple
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}
with local CM-type
\Phi=(d_{\varphi})_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}
. Then the action of
g\in{\cal{I}}_{L}
on
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})
coincides with the endomorphism
\prod_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g)^{-d_{\varphi}}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
.

Proof.

This follows from the computations in
5.4
,
5.10
and
5.2
by observing that
{\cal{I}}_{L}
acts trivially on
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},A_{v})
, because its generator
(c_{i,j})_{i,j}
is defined over the maximal unramified extension of
L
.
∎

5.12
.

To compute the absolute value
\bigl|\int_{u}\omega\bigr|_{v}
we again treat each factor
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0}
and
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
separately. We begin with
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
and set
i:=i(\varphi)
. Let
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}:=1\in\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. It is a generator as
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-module as in Definition
4.10
, and is mapped under the period isomorphism of
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
from (
5.4
) to

h_{v,{\rm dR}}^{-1}(\omega^{\circ}_{\psi})=(0,\ldots,\Bigl(\bigl(y_{i(\psi)}-\varphi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)^{\delta_{j(\psi),j(\varphi)}}\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i(\psi)},\varphi(y_{i(\psi)})})\Bigr)^{\delta_{i(\psi),i(\varphi)}},\ldots,0)\cdot\check{u}\,,

(5.7)

where the non-zero entry is in component
\psi
. We denote this entry by
\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)
. It is analogous to Colmez’s
[
Col93
, Théorème I.2.1]
element of
{\mathbf{B}}_{\rm dR}
with the same name. It satisfies the following

Theorem 5.13
.

Let
\varphi,\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
satisfy
i(\varphi)=i(\psi)=:i
and assume that
E_{v,i}
is separable over
Q_{v}
. Then the element

\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\;:=\;\bigl(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)^{\delta_{j(\psi),j(\varphi)}}\cdot\hat{\sigma}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})\;\in\;{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}\;=\;{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}

satisfies

(a)

\hat{v}\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)=1
if
\varphi=\psi
and
\hat{v}\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)=0
if
\varphi\neq\psi
.

(b)

v\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)\;=\;\begin{cases}\tfrac{1}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}})&\text{ if}\quad\varphi=\psi,\\[5.69054pt]
\tfrac{1}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}+v\big(\psi(y_{i})-\varphi(y_{i})\big)&\text{ if}\quad\varphi\neq\psi\text{ and }j(\varphi)=j(\psi),\\[8.53581pt]
\tfrac{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}&\text{ if}\quad j(\varphi)\neq j(\psi)\,,\end{cases}

where
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}}
is the different of
\psi(E_{v,i})
over
Q_{v}
.

(c)

If
g\in{\cal{I}}_{L}
, then
g\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)=\psi\bigl(\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g)\bigr)\cdot\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)
. Note that if
L
is separable over
Q_{v}
then
\psi(\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g)\bigr)=\psi\bigl(\varphi^{-1}(N_{L/\varphi(E_{v,i})}\chi_{L}(g))\bigr)
.

(d)

Let
u\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v}):=\Hom_{A_{v}}\bigl(\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v}),A_{v}\bigr)
be a generator as
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-module and let
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
be an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
subject to the conditions in Definition
4.10
, that is subject to
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\;{\rm mod}\;y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})\in\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L)
being an
R
-generator of the free
R
-module of rank one
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)\big/(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}))\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
. Moreover, let
D_{\psi}
be a generator as
\psi({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}})
-module of the different
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}}
. Then

\int_{u}\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\;:=\;u\otimes{\rm id}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}}\bigl(h^{-1}_{v,{\rm dR}}(\omega^{\circ}_{\psi})\bigr)\;\in\;{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}

equals
\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\cdot D_{\psi}^{-1}
up to multiplication by an element of
R^{\times}+(z-\zeta)\cdot L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
.

Remark 5.14
.

Note that in contrast to the number field case
[
Col93
, Théorème I.2.1]
the element
\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\in{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
is by
(a)
,
(b)
and
(c)
uniquely determined only up to multiplication by an element of
{\cal{O}}_{{\widetilde{L}}}^{\times}+(z-\zeta)\cdot{\widetilde{L}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
, where
{\widetilde{L}}
is the completion of the compositum of
{\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{\rm alg}
with the perfect closure of
L
in
Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
, because the fixed field of
{\cal{I}}_{L}
in
{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
equals
{\widetilde{L}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
by the Ax-Sen-Tate theorem
[
Ax70
]
.

Proof of Theorem
5.13
.

In
5.3
we have seen that the coefficients of the series
\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\ell_{n}y_{i}^{n}
satisfy
v(\ell_{n})=v(\varphi(y_{i}))\cdot{\tilde{q}_{i}^{-n}/(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}
. From
v(\psi(y_{i}))=1/e_{i}=v(\varphi(y_{i}))
it follows that the evaluation of
\hat{\sigma}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})\big|_{y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\ell_{n}^{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}}\psi(y_{i})^{n}
at
y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})
satisfies

v(\ell_{n}^{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}}\psi(y_{i})^{n})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{e_{i}}\cdot\Bigl(n+\tfrac{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}}{\tilde{q}_{i}^{n}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}\Bigr).

(5.8)

Since
0\leq(j(\psi),j(\varphi))\leq f_{i}-1
the second fraction in the parenthesis is strictly smaller than
1
, and so the valuations in (
5.8
) are strictly increasing with
n
and attain their minimum
\tfrac{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}
for
n=0
. This shows that
\hat{\sigma}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})\big|_{y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})}
is non-zero in
L
and

v\Bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})\big|_{y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})}\Bigr)\;=\;\tfrac{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}\,.

In particular the valuation
\hat{v}\bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})\bigr)=0
.

(a)
Lemma
A.1
implies that in the
\psi
-component of
E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
we have
\ord_{y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})}=\ord_{z-\zeta}
. If
j(\varphi)=j(\psi)
, that is
\varphi|_{{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}}=\psi|_{{\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}}
then
\varphi\neq\psi
implies
\psi(y_{i})-\varphi(y_{i})\neq 0
in
L
, because
E_{v,i}={\mathbb{F}}_{\tilde{v}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}y_{i}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
. Therefore the valuation
\hat{v}
of
y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})=(\psi(y_{i})-\varphi(y_{i}))+(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}))
equals zero for
\varphi\neq\psi
and
j(\varphi)=j(\psi)
. This implies
(a)
.

(b)
We will calculate
v\bigl((\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)
in three different cases separately as follows.

Case1:
\psi=\varphi
. In this case
\hat{v}\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)=1
and so

\displaystyle v\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v\bigl(\Bigl(\tfrac{y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})}{z-\zeta}\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}\Bigr)\Big|_{y_{i}=\varphi(y_{i})}\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v\bigl(\tfrac{y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})}{z-\zeta}\big|_{y_{i}=\varphi(y_{i})}\bigr)+v\bigl(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}|_{y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})}\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}})+\tfrac{1}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}

by Corollary
4.7
.

Case 2:
\psi\neq\varphi
and
j(\psi)=j(\varphi)
. In this case
\hat{v}\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)=0
and so

\displaystyle v\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v\Bigl(\bigl((y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}))\cdot\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}\bigr)\big|_{y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v\bigl(\psi(y_{i})-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)+v\bigl(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})}|_{y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})}\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v\bigl(\psi(y_{i})-\varphi(y_{i})\bigr)+\tfrac{1}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}\,.

Case 3:
j(\psi)\neq j(\varphi)
. In this case
\hat{v}\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)=0
and so

v\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)\;=\;v\Bigl(\hat{\sigma}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}(\ell^{\scriptscriptstyle+}_{y_{i},\varphi(y_{i})})|_{y_{i}=\psi(y_{i})}\Bigr)\;=\;\tfrac{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))}}{e_{i}(\tilde{q}_{i}-1)}\,.

(c)
For the
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-basis
\check{u}
of
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
from (
5.3
) we have seen in
5.10
that
g(\check{u})=\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g)^{-1}\cdot\check{u}
and
g\bigl(0,\ldots,\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi),\ldots,0\bigr)\cdot g(\check{u})=h_{v,{\rm dR}}^{-1}(g(\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}))

=h_{v,{\rm dR}}^{-1}(\omega^{\circ}_{\psi})=\bigl(0,\ldots,\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi),\ldots,0\bigr)\cdot\check{u}
. Thus
g
acts on the coefficient
\bigl(0,\ldots,\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi),\ldots,0\bigr)
as multiplication with
\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g)
and on its
\psi
-component
\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)
by multiplication with
\psi(\chi_{E_{v},\varphi}(g))
.

(d)
Again we consider the
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-basis
\check{u}
of
\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
from (
5.3
). Let
D=(D_{i})_{i}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}=\prod_{i}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
be a generator of the different
{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v}/Q_{v}}=\prod_{i}{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}=D\cdot{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
and let
c=(c_{i})_{i}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}=\prod_{i}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
be the element(s) from Lemma
5.15
below for which the pairing
\langle\,.\,,\,.\,\rangle\colon\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})\times\Koh_{v}^{1}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})\to A_{v}
takes the value
\langle a\,u\,,\,b\,\check{u}\rangle=\Tr_{E_{v}/Q_{v}}(abcD^{-1})
for
a,b\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
. If
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}=1
is the generator from
5.12
then

{\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\;=\;\Tr_{E_{v}/Q_{v}}\bigl(0,\ldots,\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\cdot\psi(cD^{-1}),\ldots,0\bigr)\;=\;\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\cdot\psi(c_{i}D_{i}^{-1})\,.

Any other generator
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
differs from
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}=1
by multiplication by an element

x\;\in\;R^{\times}+(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}))\cdot L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\subset E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\,.

Under the pairing
\langle\,.\,,\,.\,\rangle
this leads to
\int_{u}\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}=\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\cdot\psi(c_{i}xD_{i}^{-1})
with
\psi(D_{i})=D_{\psi}
and

\psi(c_{i}x)\;\in\;R^{\times}+(y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}))\cdot L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\;=\;R^{\times}+(z-\zeta)\cdot L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\,.

∎

It remains to record the following well known

Lemma 5.15
.

If
E_{v,i}/Q_{v}
is separable let
D_{i}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
be a generator of the different
{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}=D_{i}\cdot{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
. Then for any perfect pairing
\langle\,.\,,\,.\,\rangle\colon{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\times{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\to A_{v}
satisfying
\langle a,b\rangle=\langle ab,1\rangle=\langle 1,ab\rangle
, there is an element
c_{i}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
with
\langle a,b\rangle=\Tr_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}(abc_{i}D_{i}^{-1})
.

Proof.

The set of bilinear forms
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\times{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\to A_{v}
equals
\Hom_{A_{v}}({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\otimes_{A_{v}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}},A_{v})
and the condition
\langle a,b\rangle=\langle ab,1\rangle=\langle 1,ab\rangle
implies that
\langle\,.\,,\,.\,\rangle
lies in
\Hom_{A_{v}}({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\otimes_{{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}}{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}},A_{v})=\Hom_{A_{v}}({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}},A_{v})
. The condition that
\langle\,.\,,\,.\,\rangle
is perfect implies that

{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;\Hom_{A_{v}}({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}},A_{v}),\quad a\;\longmapsto\;[\,b\mapsto\langle a,b\rangle\,]

(5.9)

is an isomorphism of
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
-modules. On the other hand, by definition of the different in
[
Ser79
, § III.3]
, there are also isomorphisms of
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
-modules

{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}^{-1}\;=\;D_{i}^{-1}\cdot{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;\Hom_{A_{v}}({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}},A_{v}),\quad\tilde{a}D_{i}^{-1}\;\longmapsto\;[\,b\mapsto\Tr_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}(\tilde{a}bD_{i}^{-1})\,]

for
\tilde{a}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
, and

{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;\Hom_{A_{v}}({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}},A_{v}),\quad\tilde{a}\;\longmapsto\;[\,b\mapsto\Tr_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}(\tilde{a}bD_{i}^{-1})\,]\,.

(5.10)

Comparing (
5.9
) and (
5.10
) yields a unit
c_{i}\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
with
\tilde{a}=c_{i}a
and
\langle a,b\rangle=\Tr_{E_{v,i}/Q_{v}}(abc_{i}D_{i}^{-1})
.
∎

5.16
.

Analogously to
[
Col93
, § I.2]
, we can give a uniform formula for
v\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)
by introducing certain measures on
{\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}}
. Let
{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
be the
{\mathbb{Q}}
-vector space of locally constant functions
a\colon{\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}}\to{\mathbb{Q}}
. If
K
is a finite separable extension of
Q_{v}
, and
\varphi,\psi\in H_{K}
, let
a_{K,\varphi,\psi}\in{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
be the function given by

a_{K,\varphi,\psi}(g)\;:=\;\begin{cases}1&\text{if}\quad g\varphi=\psi,\\
0&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Note that the
a_{K,\varphi,\psi}
span
{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
.

If
L\subset Q_{v}^{\rm sep}
is a finite Galois extension of
Q_{v}
, let
\mu_{L}\in{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
be the function given by the formula

\mu_{L}(g)\;:=\;\begin{cases}0&\text{if}\quad g\notin{\cal{I}}_{Q_{v}},\\
-v(g(\pi_{L})-\pi_{L})&\text{if}\quad g\in{\cal{I}}_{Q_{v}}\ \text{and}\ g(\pi_{L})\neq\pi_{L},\\
v({\mathfrak{D}}_{L/Q_{v}})&\text{if}\quad g\in{\cal{I}}_{Q_{v}}\ \text{and}\ g(\pi_{L})=\pi_{L},\end{cases}

where
\pi_{L}
is a uniformizer of
L
and
{\mathfrak{D}}_{L/Q_{v}}
is the different of
L
over
Q_{v}
.

Moreover, we let
e_{K}
be the ramification index of
K
over
Q_{v}
and
f_{K}
the degree of the residue field of
K
over
{\mathbb{F}}_{v}
. We let
W_{L}^{n}:=\bigl\{g\in{\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}}\colon g(x)\equiv x^{q_{v}^{n}}\;{\rm mod}\;{\mathfrak{m}}_{Q_{v}^{\rm alg}}\kern 5.0pt\forall\;x\in{\cal{O}}_{Q_{v}^{\rm alg}}\bigr\}/{\cal{I}}_{L}
. It is in bijection with
\{g\in\Gal(L/Q_{v})\colon g(x)\equiv x^{q_{v}^{n}}\;{\rm mod}\;(\pi_{L})\}
under the map
{\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}}\twoheadrightarrow\Gal(L/Q_{v})
.

Lemma 5.17
.

Let
K,L\subset Q_{v}^{\rm sep}
be finite separable extensions of
Q_{v}
with
L
finite Galois over
Q_{v}
containing all the conjugates of
K
, and let
\varphi,\psi\in H_{K}
. The function
a_{K,\varphi,\psi}
is constant modulo
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
and hence can be considered as a function on
G_{L}:=\Gal(L/Q_{v})
. Then

\sum_{g\in G_{L}}a_{K,\varphi,\psi}(g)\cdot\mu_{L}(g)\;=\;\begin{cases}0&\text{if}\quad j(\varphi)\neq j(\psi),\\
v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(K)/Q_{v}})&\text{if}\quad\varphi=\psi,\\
-v\bigl(\psi(\pi_{K})-\varphi(\pi_{K})\bigr)&\text{if}\quad j(\varphi)=j(\psi)\ \text{and}\ \varphi\neq\psi,\end{cases}

and

\frac{1}{e_{L}}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{g\in W^{n}_{L}}\frac{a_{K,\varphi,\psi}(g)}{q_{v}^{ns}}\;=\;\frac{1}{e_{K}}\;\frac{q_{v}^{(j(\psi),j(\varphi))s}}{q_{v}^{f_{K}s}-1}.

In particular, the left hand side of both equations does not depend on the choice of
L
.

Proof.

The proof follows in the same way as
[
Col93
, Lemma I.2.4]
.
∎

Since the
a_{K,\varphi,\psi}
generate the vector space
{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
, we get the following proposition.

Proposition 5.18
.

There exist
{\mathbb{Q}}
-linear homomorphisms
Z_{v}(\,.\,,s):{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})\to{\mathbb{C}}
if
s\in{\mathbb{C}}
and
\mu_{{\rm Art},v}:{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})\to{\mathbb{Q}}
defined by the following formulas: if
a\in{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
and if
L\subset Q_{v}^{\rm sep}
is a finite Galois extension of
Q_{v}
such that
a
is constant modulo
{\mathscr{G}}_{L}
, then

\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a)\;=\;\sum_{g\in G_{L}}a(g)\cdot\mu_{L}(g)

with
G_{L}:=\Gal(L/Q_{v})
, and
Z_{v}(a,s)
is obtained by meromorphic extension from the following formula, valid for
{\cal{R}}e(s)>0
:

\hskip 150.79968ptZ_{v}(a,s)\;=\;\frac{1}{e_{L}}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{g\in W^{n}_{L}}\frac{a(g)}{q_{v}^{ns}}\hskip 150.79968pt\qed

Remark 5.19
.

If
V
is a finite dimensional
{\mathbb{C}}
-vector space,
\rho\colon{\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}}\to\Aut_{\mathbb{C}}(V)
is a continuous complex representation of
{\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}}
, and if
\chi\in{\cal{C}}^{0}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Q}}{\mathbb{C}}
is the character of
\rho
, then
\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(\chi)
is nothing else than the degree at
v
of the conductor
{\mathfrak{f}}_{\chi}
of
\chi
; cf.
[
Ser79
, Chapter VI, § 2]
, where
\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(\chi)
is denoted by
f(\chi)
. And if
W
is the sub vector space of
V
stable by
I_{Q_{v}}
, we have

Z_{v}(\chi,a)\log q_{v}\;=\;-\frac{d}{ds}\log\Bigl(\det(1-q_{v}^{-s}\rho(\Frob_{L/Q_{v}})|_{W})^{-1}\Bigr)

by
[
Tat84
, Chapter 0, § 4]
or
[
Ros02
, Lemma 9.14]
. So the linear maps
\mu_{{\rm Art},v}
and
Z_{v}(\,.\,,s)
coincide with the maps with the same names in Definition
1.2
in the introduction.

As a direct consequence of Theorem
5.13
, Proposition
5.18
and Lemma
5.17
we get the following

Theorem 5.20
.

If
\varphi,\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
satisfy
i(\varphi)=i(\psi)=:i
and
E_{v,i}
is separable over
Q_{v}
then

v\bigl(\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi})\,,

where we set
a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi}:=a_{E_{v,i},\psi,\varphi}\colon g\mapsto\delta_{g\psi,\varphi}
.
∎

Definition 5.21
.

If
u\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},Q_{v}):=\Hom_{A_{v}}\bigl(\Koh^{1}_{v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v}),Q_{v}\bigr)
is an
E_{v}
-generator there is an
a\in E_{v}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
, unique up to multiplication with an element of
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
, such that
a^{-1}u
is an
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-generator of
\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})
. Then we define the valuation
v_{\psi}(u):=v\bigl(\psi(a)\bigr)\in{\mathbb{Z}}
.
(NOTE THAT
v_{\psi}(u)\;\in\;{\mathbb{Q}}
IN GENERAL; SEE ERRATUM
B.1
)

Note that if
{\underline{M\!}\,}=(M,\tau_{M})
is a uniformizable
A
-motive over
L
with good model
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
and
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{v}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}})
is the local shtuka at
v
associated with
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
as in Example
3.2
, then for an
E
-generator
u\in\Koh_{1,{\rm Betti}}({\underline{M\!}\,},Q)
the present definition of
v_{\psi}\bigl(h_{{\rm Betti},v}(u)\bigr)
coincides with the definition of
v_{\psi}(u)
from (
1.12
).

Corollary 5.22
.

Let
\varphi,\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
with
i(\varphi)=i(\psi)=:i
and assume that
E_{v,i}
is separable over
Q_{v}
. Let
u\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},Q_{v})
be a generator as
E_{v}
-module and let
\omega_{\psi}
be an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. Then
\int_{u}\omega_{\psi}:=u\otimes{\rm id}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}}\bigl(h^{-1}_{v,{\rm dR}}(\omega_{\psi})\bigr)
has valuation

v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi})-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})+v(\omega_{\psi})+v_{\psi}(u)\,,

where
v(\omega_{\psi})
and
v_{\psi}(u)
were defined in Definitions
4.10
and
5.21
, and
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}}
is the different.

Proof.

Let
a\in E_{v}
be such that
u^{\circ}:=a^{-1}u
is an
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-generator of
\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},A_{v})
and let
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
be an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
such that
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\;{\rm mod}\;y_{i}-\varphi(y_{i})\in\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L)
is an
R
-generator of
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},R)
. Let
x\in L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
such that
\omega_{\psi}=x\cdot\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
. Moreover, let
D_{\psi}
be a generator as
\psi({\cal{O}}_{E_{v}})
-module of the different
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}}
. Then

{\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}\;=\;(a\otimes 1)x\cdot{\textstyle\int_{u^{\circ}}}\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\;=\;(a\otimes 1)x\cdot\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\cdot D_{\psi}^{-1}\;\in\;{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}

up to multiplication by an element of
R^{\times}+(z-\zeta)\cdot L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
by Theorem
5.13
(d)
. The element
(a\otimes 1)x\in E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
lies in the
\psi
-component
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
of the product decomposition (
A.1
), and in that component
a\otimes 1
is congruent to
\psi(a)
modulo
y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})
. Therefore

v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}\bigr)\;=\;v\bigl(\psi(a)x\cdot\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)\cdot D_{\psi}^{-1}\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi})-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})+v(\omega_{\psi})+v_{\psi}(u)\,.

by Theorem
5.20
.
∎

To compute
v(\int_{u}\omega_{\psi})
for general
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
we need the following

Definition 5.23
.

Let
E_{v}
be separable over
Q_{v}
and let
\Phi=(d_{\varphi})_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}
be a local CM-type. For
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
let
a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}\in{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
and
a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}\in{\cal{C}}^{0}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
be given by the formulas

\displaystyle a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}(g)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle{\textstyle\sum\limits_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}}d_{\varphi}\cdot a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi}(g)\kern 5.0pt=\kern 5.0ptd_{g\psi}\qquad\text{and}

(5.11)

\displaystyle a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}(g)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\#H_{L}}\textstyle\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{L}}d_{\eta^{-1}g\eta\psi}\,.

(5.12)

Note that
a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}
and
a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}
factor through
\Gal(E_{v}^{\rm nor}/Q_{v})
where
E_{v}^{\rm nor}
is the Galois closure of
\psi(E_{v})
in
Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
. In particular,
a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}
does not depend on the field
L
provided
\psi(E_{v})\subset L
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
.

These functions are the local counterparts to the functions
a_{E,\psi,\Phi}\in{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q},{\mathbb{Q}})
and
a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi}\in{\cal{C}}^{0}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q},{\mathbb{Q}})
which were defined in (
1.10
) and (
1.11
). The membership in
{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
, respectively
{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q},{\mathbb{Q}})
is indicated by the index which gives reference to the
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}
, respectively the
Q
-algebra
E
. In fact, if
E_{v}=E\otimes_{Q}Q_{v}
and hence
H_{E_{v}}=H_{E}
, then
a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}
is equal to the image of
a_{E,\psi,\Phi}
under the map
{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q},{\mathbb{Q}})\to{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
from Definition
1.2
. However, this is in general not true for
a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}
and
a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi}
, because if
L
is the closure of
K
in
Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
, then
H_{L}
is in general strictly contained in
H_{K}
.

For general
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
we can now prove the following

Theorem 5.24
.

Let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
be a local shtuka over
R
with complex multiplication by the ring of integers
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
in a commutative, semi-simple, separable
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}
with local CM-type
\Phi
, and assume that
\psi(E_{v})\subset L
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
and that
L
is separable over
Q_{v}
. Let
u\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},Q_{v})
be an
E_{v}
-generator and let
\omega_{\psi}
be an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. Then the period
\int_{u}\omega_{\psi}:=u\otimes{\rm id}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}}\bigl(h^{-1}_{v,{\rm dR}}(\omega_{\psi})\bigr)
has valuation

v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi})-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})+v(\omega_{\psi})+v_{\psi}(u)\,,

where
v(\omega_{\psi})
and
v_{\psi}(u)
were defined in Definitions
4.10
and
5.21
, and
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}}
is the different.

Proof.

As in
5.1
the local shtuka
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
is isomorphic to the tensor product
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0}\otimes\bigotimes_{\varphi}{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}{}^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}
over
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}
. Let
i:=i(\psi)
and
j:=j(\psi)
. For every
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi}
we fix the
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi,\varphi}:=1\in\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. In addition, we let
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi,0}:=1\in\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. Then we can take the tensor product
\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}:=\omega^{\circ}_{\psi,0}\otimes\bigotimes\limits_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}(\omega^{\circ}_{\psi,\varphi})^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}
in

\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\;\cong\;\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},0},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\otimes\bigotimes\limits_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})^{\otimes d_{\varphi}}.

It is an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator as in Definition
4.10
. Let
x\in L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
be such that
\omega_{\psi}=x\cdot\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
, and let further
a\in E_{v}
be such that
u^{\circ}:=a^{-1}u
is an
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
-generator of
\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},A_{v})
. Moreover, let
D_{\psi}
be a generator as
\psi({\cal{O}}_{E_{v}})
-module of the different
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v,i})/Q_{v}}
. Then (
5.5
), (
5.7
) and Lemma
5.15
imply that

{\textstyle\int_{u^{\circ}}}\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}\;=\;D_{\psi}^{-1}\cdot\varepsilon_{i,j}c_{i,j}^{-1}\prod_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)^{d_{\varphi}}

up to multiplication by an element of
R^{\times}+(z-\zeta)\cdot L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. Since
\varepsilon_{i,j}c_{i,j}^{-1}\in\bigl({\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\otimes_{A_{v}}{\cal{O}}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\bigr)^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
we conclude as in the proof of Corollary
5.22
that
{\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}=(a\otimes 1)x\cdot{\textstyle\int_{u^{\circ}}}\omega^{\circ}_{\psi}
and

\displaystyle v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v\Bigl(D_{\psi}^{-1}\cdot\psi(a)x\cdot\varepsilon_{i,j}c_{i,j}^{-1}\prod_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}\Omega(E_{v},\varphi,\psi)^{d_{\varphi}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v(\omega_{\psi})+v_{\psi}(u)-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})+\sum_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}\bigl(Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi})\bigr)\cdot d_{\varphi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi})-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})+v(\omega_{\psi})+v_{\psi}(u)\,,

because
i(\varphi)\neq i(\psi)
implies that
a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi}(g)=\delta_{g\psi,\varphi}=0
for all
g\in{\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}}
.
∎

Corollary 5.25
.

Keep the situation of Theorem
5.24
. For every
\eta\in H_{L}
note that
i(\eta\psi)=i(\psi)
, let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta}
and
\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}\in\Koh^{\eta\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\eta\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
be obtained by extension of scalars via
\eta
, and choose an
E_{v}
-generator
u_{\eta}\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta},Q_{v})
. Then

\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\#H_{L}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{L}}v({\textstyle\int_{u_{\eta}}\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}})\;=\;Z_{v}(a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi})-\dfrac{v({\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})}{[\psi(E_{v}):Q_{v}]}+\tfrac{1}{\#H_{L}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{L}}\bigl(v(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})+v_{\eta\psi}(u_{\eta})\bigr)\,,

where
{\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}}\subset A_{v}
is the discriminant of the field extension
\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}
.

Proof.

Since
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta}
has complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
with local CM-type
\eta\Phi:=(d^{\prime}_{\varphi})_{\varphi\in H_{E_{v}}}
with
d^{\prime}_{\varphi}=d_{\eta^{-1}\varphi}
, Theorem
5.24
implies

v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u_{\eta}}}\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi})-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\eta\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})+v(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})+v_{\eta\psi}(u_{\eta})\,.

(5.13)

We sum over all
\eta\in H_{L}
, divide by
\#H_{L}=[L:Q_{v}]
, and observe that
a_{E_{v},\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi}(g)=d^{\prime}_{g\eta\psi}=d_{\eta^{-1}g\eta\psi}
and
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\eta\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}}=\eta({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})
, and hence

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{L}}v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\eta\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})

\displaystyle\;=\;\textstyle v\Bigl(\,\prod\limits_{\eta\in H_{L}}\eta({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})\Bigr)\;=\;v\bigl(N_{L/Q_{v}}({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})\bigr)

\displaystyle\;=\;v\Bigl(N_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}}\bigl(N_{L/\psi(E_{v})}({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})\bigr)\Bigr)\;=\;[L:\psi(E_{v})]\cdot v({\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})\,.

This proves the corollary.
∎

Finally we are ready to give the

Proof of Theorem
1.3
.

The proof proceeds like the one of the previous corollary applied to
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}:={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{v}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}})
for a model
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
of
{\underline{M\!}\,}
with good reduction. Setting
E_{v}:=E\otimes_{Q}Q_{v}
, still
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta}:={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{v}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}}^{\eta})
has complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
with local CM-type
\eta\Phi:=(d^{\prime}_{\varphi})_{\varphi\in H_{E}}
with
d^{\prime}_{\varphi}=d_{\eta^{-1}\varphi}
, where
\eta
runs over all elements in
H_{K}
. To translate the global situation to the local one, we also use the elements
h_{{\rm Betti},v}(u_{\eta})\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta},Q_{v})
, and
\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}\otimes_{K}L\in\Koh^{\eta\psi}({\underline{M\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\eta\psi)}-\eta\psi(y_{i(\eta\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})=\Koh^{\eta\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\eta\psi)}-\eta\psi(y_{i(\eta\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. Then
a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi}\in{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q_{v}},{\mathbb{Q}})
is the image of
a_{E,\psi,\Phi}\in{\cal{C}}({\mathscr{G}}_{Q},{\mathbb{Q}})
, and (
5.13
) takes the form

v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u_{\eta}}}\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E,\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E,\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi})-v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\eta\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})+v(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})+v_{\eta\psi}(u_{\eta})\,,

(5.14)

because the value of
v_{\eta\psi}\bigl(h_{{\rm Betti},v}(u_{\eta})\bigr)
from Definition
5.21
used in (
5.13
), coincides with the value of
v_{\eta\psi}(u_{\eta})
from (
1.12
). This time we sum over all
\eta\in H_{K}
, divide by
\#H_{K}=[K:Q]
, and observe that
a_{E,\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi}(g)=d^{\prime}_{g\eta\psi}=d_{\eta^{-1}g\eta\psi}
, and hence

\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\#H_{K}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}Z_{v}(a_{E,\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E,\,\eta\psi,\,\eta\Phi})\;=\;Z_{v}(a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi})\,.

(5.15)

For every place
w
of the field
\psi(E)
above
v
let
\psi(E)_{w}
be the completion. Then
\psi(E)\otimes_{Q}Q_{v}=\prod_{w|v}\psi(E)_{w}
. Via the fixed inclusion
Q^{\rm alg}\subset Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
we consider every
\eta\in H_{K}
as a morphism
\eta\colon K\to Q^{\rm alg}\subset Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
. The induced morphism
\eta\otimes{\rm id}_{Q_{v}}\colon K\otimes_{Q}Q_{v}\to Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
, when restricted to a morphism
\psi(E)\otimes_{Q}Q_{v}\to Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
factors over
\psi(E)_{w}
for a unique
w
which we denote by
w(\eta)
. We set
H_{K,w}:=\{\eta\in H_{K}\colon w(\eta)=w\}
and consider the map

H_{K,w}\longrightarrow H_{\psi(E)_{w}}\,,\quad\eta\longmapsto(\eta\otimes{\rm id}_{Q_{v}})|_{\psi(E)_{w}}\;.

(5.16)

The map is surjective, because every element of
H_{\psi(E)_{w}}
can be restricted to a
Q
-homomorphism
\psi(E)\to Q^{\rm alg}\subset Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
, which extends to a
Q
-homomorphism
(\eta\colon K\to Q^{\rm alg})\in H_{K}
that automatically lies in
H_{K,w}
. We claim that two elements
\eta,\tilde{\eta}\in H_{K,w}
have the same image under the map (
5.16
) if and only if
\tilde{\eta}=\eta\circ\alpha
for an element
\alpha\in\Gal\bigl(K/\psi(E)\bigr)
. Indeed the latter condition is sufficient, because
\alpha\otimes{\rm id}_{Q_{v}}
induces the identity on
\psi(E)_{w}
. To see that it is necessary let
\eta,\tilde{\eta}\in H_{K,w}\subset H_{K}=\Gal(K/Q)
have the same image. Then their restrictions to
\psi(E)\subset\psi(E)_{w}
coincide, and hence
\alpha:=\eta^{-1}\circ\tilde{\eta}\in\Gal(K/Q)
lies in
\Gal\bigl(K/\psi(E)\bigr)
as claimed. For every
\eta\in H_{K,w}
the different
{\mathfrak{D}}_{\eta\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}}
equals
(\eta\otimes{\rm id}_{Q_{v}})({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E)_{w}/Q_{v}})
and only depends on the image of
\eta
under the map (
5.16
). Therefore, we compute

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K,w}}v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\eta\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})

\displaystyle\;=\;\textstyle v\Bigl(\,\prod\limits_{\eta\in H_{K,w}}(\eta\otimes{\rm id}_{Q_{v}})({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E)_{w}/Q_{v}})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\;=\;v\Bigl(N_{\psi(E)_{w}/Q_{v}}({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E)_{w}/Q_{v}})^{\#\Gal\bigl(K/\psi(E)\bigr)}\Bigr)\;=\;[K:\psi(E)]\cdot v({\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi(E)_{w}/Q_{v}})\,.

Summing over all
w|v
and using that
\sum_{w|v}v({\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi(E)_{w}/Q_{v}})=v({\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi(E)/Q})
by
[
Ser79
, § III.4, Corollary to Proposition 10]
we obtain from (
5.14
) and (
5.15
)

\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\#H_{K}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}v({\textstyle\int_{u_{\eta}}\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}})\;=\;Z_{v}(a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi})-\dfrac{v({\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi(E)/Q})}{[\psi(E):Q]}+\tfrac{1}{\#H_{K}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}\bigl(v(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})+v_{\eta\psi}(u_{\eta})\bigr).

This proves Theorem
1.3
.
∎

Appendix A
Appendix: Product Decompositions of Certain Rings

In this appendix we establish certain product decompositions for the rings used in this article. We begin with the following

Lemma A.1
.

Let
k
be a field and let
z=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}b_{n}y^{n}\in k{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
. Let
\psi\colon k{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\to R
be a ring homomorphism into a
k
-algebra
R
. Then in
k{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}{\widehat{\otimes}}_{k,\psi}R:=\displaystyle\lim_{\underset{n}{\longleftarrow}}k{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}/(y^{n})\otimes_{k,\psi}R\cong R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
the fraction
\tfrac{z\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(z)}{y\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(y)}
exists and is congruent to
1\otimes\psi\bigl(\tfrac{dz}{dy}\bigr)
modulo
y\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(y)
.

Proof.

The lemma follows from the computation

\displaystyle z\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\bigl(b_{n}y^{n}\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(b_{n})\psi(y)^{n}\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\bigl(1\otimes\psi(b_{n})\bigr)\cdot\sum_{\nu=0}^{n-1}\bigl(y^{\nu}\otimes\psi(y)^{n-1-\nu}\bigr)\cdot\bigl(y\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(y)\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigl(y\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(y)\bigr)\cdot\sum_{\nu=0}^{\infty}y^{\nu}\otimes\psi\Bigl(\sum_{n=\nu+1}^{\infty}b_{n}y^{n-1-\nu}\Bigr)\,,

where the second factor converges in
k{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}{\widehat{\otimes}}_{k,\psi}R
. Modulo
y\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(y)
this factor equals

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\bigl(1\otimes\psi(b_{n})\bigr)\cdot\sum_{\nu=0}^{n-1}\bigl(y^{\nu}\otimes\psi(y)^{n-1-\nu}\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\bigl(1\otimes\psi(b_{n})\bigr)\cdot n\bigl(1\otimes\psi(y)^{n-1}\bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1\otimes\psi\Bigl(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}nb_{n}y^{n-1}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1\otimes\psi\Bigl(\frac{dz}{dy}\Bigr)\,.

∎

We need the following well known fact from field theory. For the convenience of the reader we include a proof.

Lemma A.2
.

Let
E
be a finite field extension of
Q
(or of
Q_{v}
) of inseparability degree
p^{m}
. Then the separable closure
E^{\prime}
of
Q
(resp. of
Q_{v}
) in
E
equals
E^{p^{m}}:=\{x^{p^{m}}\colon x\in E\}
. If
y
is a uniformizing parameter at a place
\tilde{v}
of
E
then
y^{\prime}:=y^{p^{m}}
is a uniformizing parameter at the place
\tilde{v}^{\prime}
of
E^{\prime}
below
\tilde{v}
and
E=E^{\prime}(y)=E^{\prime}[X]/(X^{p^{m}}-y^{\prime})
.

Proof.

This is due to the fact that
Q
has transcendence degree one over
{\mathbb{F}}_{q}
, respectively that
Q_{v}
is a discretely valued field. Namely, consider the case for
Q_{v}
. Then
E=k{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}y{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
where
k
is the finite residue field of
E
. Clearly
E^{p^{m}}=k{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.49994pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}y^{\prime}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.49994pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}
and
E=E^{p^{m}}(y)=E^{p^{m}}[X]/(X^{p^{m}}-y^{\prime})
, because
X^{p^{m}}-y^{\prime}
is irreducible in
E^{p^{m}}[X]
by Eisenstein. In particular
[E:E^{p^{m}}]=p^{m}
. On the other hand, the minimal polynomial
f(X)
of
y
over
E^{\prime}
is of the form
g(X^{p^{m^{\prime}}})
for a separable, irreducible polynomial
g
over
E^{\prime}
and an integer
m^{\prime}\geq 0
. Therefore the minimal polynomial of
y^{p^{m^{\prime}}}
over
E^{\prime}
is
g
and
y^{p^{m^{\prime}}}
is separable over
E^{\prime}
. This implies
y^{p^{m^{\prime}}}\in E^{\prime}
and
\deg g=1
, whence
\deg f=p^{m^{\prime}}\leq p^{m}
. Therefore
m^{\prime}\leq m
and
y^{p^{m}}\in E^{\prime}
, and hence
E^{p^{m}}\subset E^{\prime}
. Since
[E:E^{\prime}]=p^{m}=[E:E^{p^{m}}]
it follows that
E^{\prime}=E^{p^{m}}
. This proves the lemma for
Q_{v}
.

For
Q
a proof for the equality
E^{\prime}=E^{p^{m}}
can be found for example in
[
Sil86
, Chapter II, Corollary 2.12]
. If
{\cal{O}}_{E,\tilde{v}}
is the valuation ring of
E
at
\tilde{v}
then
({\cal{O}}_{E,\tilde{v}})^{p^{m}}
equals the valuation ring
{\cal{O}}_{E^{\prime},\tilde{v}^{\prime}}
of
E^{\prime}
at
\tilde{v}^{\prime}
and so
y^{\prime}
is a uniformizing parameter of
{\cal{O}}_{E^{\prime},\tilde{v}^{\prime}}
. The last equality follows from the fact that the polynomial
X^{p^{m}}-y^{\prime}\in{\cal{O}}_{E^{\prime},\tilde{v}^{\prime}}[X]
is irreducible by Eisenstein.
∎

In the next lemma we consider the embeddings
Q\hookrightarrow K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and
Q_{v}\hookrightarrow L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
given by
z_{v}\mapsto z_{v}=\zeta_{v}+(z_{v}-\zeta_{v})
.

Lemma A.3
.

Let
E=E_{1}\times\ldots\times E_{s}
be a product of finite field extensions of
Q
and let
K\subset Q^{\rm alg}
be a field extension of
Q
with
\psi(E)\subset K
for all
\psi\in H_{E}:=\Hom_{Q}(E,Q^{\rm alg})
. Let
i(\psi)
be such that
\psi
factors through
E\twoheadrightarrow E_{i(\psi)}
and let
y_{i(\psi)}\in E_{i(\psi)}
be a uniformizing parameter at a place of
E_{i(\psi)}
above
v
. Then

\displaystyle E\otimes_{Q}K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\;=\;\prod_{\psi\in H_{E}}K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\qquad\text{and}

\displaystyle E\otimes_{Q}K\;=\;\prod_{\psi\in H_{E}}K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}/\bigl(y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)^{[E_{i(\psi)}\,:\,Q]_{\rm insep}},

where
[E_{i(\psi)}:Q]_{\rm insep}
is the inseparability degree of
E_{i(\psi)}
over
Q
.

Likewise, let
E_{v}=E_{v,1}\times\ldots\times E_{v,s}
be a product of finite field extensions of
Q_{v}
and let
L\subset Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
be a field extension of
Q_{v}
with
\psi(E_{v})\subset L
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}:=\Hom_{Q_{v}}(E_{v},Q_{v}^{\rm alg})
. Let
i(\psi)
be such that
\psi
factors through
E\twoheadrightarrow E_{v,i(\psi)}
and let
y_{i(\psi)}\in E_{v,i(\psi)}
be a uniformizing parameter. Then

\displaystyle E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\;=\;\prod_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\qquad\text{and}

(A.1)

\displaystyle E_{v}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L\;=\;\prod_{\psi\in H_{E_{v}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}/\bigl(y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)^{[E_{v,i(\psi)}\,:\,Q_{v}]_{\rm insep}},

(A.2)

where
[E_{v,i(\psi)}:Q_{v}]_{\rm insep}
is the inseparability degree of
E_{v,i(\psi)}
over
Q_{v}
.

Proof.

Fix a
\psi
, set
i:=i(\psi)
and let
E^{\prime}_{i}
, respectively
E^{\prime}_{v,i}
be the separable closure of
Q
in
E_{i}
, respectively of
Q_{v}
in
E_{v,i}
. Then
H_{E_{i}}=H_{E^{\prime}_{i}}
, respectively
H_{E_{v,i}}=H_{E^{\prime}_{v,i}}
, and

\textstyle E^{\prime}_{i}\otimes_{Q}K\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;\prod\limits_{\psi\in H_{E_{i}}}K\,,\qquad\text{respectively}\qquad E^{\prime}_{v,i}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}\;\prod\limits_{\psi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}L\,.

(A.3)

Let
p^{m}:=[E_{i}:Q]_{\rm insep}=[E_{i}:E^{\prime}_{i}]
, respectively
p^{m}:=[E_{v,i}:Q_{v}]_{\rm insep}=[E_{v,i}:E^{\prime}_{v,i}]
, and let
y^{\prime}_{i}:=y_{i}^{p^{m}}
. Then Lemma
A.2
implies that
y^{\prime}_{i}\in E^{\prime}_{i}
is a uniformizing parameter at a place above
v
. By Hensel’s lemma the decompositions (
A.3
) extend to decompositions

\displaystyle E^{\prime}_{i}\otimes_{Q}K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}

\displaystyle\textstyle\prod\limits_{\psi\in H_{E_{i}}}K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\kern 5.0pt=\kern 5.0pt\textstyle\prod\limits_{\psi\in H_{E_{i}}}K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\,,\quad\text{respectively}

\displaystyle E^{\prime}_{v,i}\otimes_{Q_{v}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}

\displaystyle\textstyle\prod\limits_{\psi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\kern 5.0pt=\kern 5.0pt\textstyle\prod\limits_{\psi\in H_{E_{v,i}}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}\,.

Here the last identifications in each line follow from
[
HJ16
, Lemma 1.2 and 1.3]
which states that both
K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}-\zeta_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
and
K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
are canonically isomorphic to the completion of the ring
{\cal{O}}_{E_{i}^{\prime}}\otimes_{{\mathbb{F}}_{q}}K
at the ideal
(a\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(a)\colon a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{i}^{\prime}})
. The identification in the second line also follows from Lemma
A.1
by observing that the derivative
\tfrac{dz_{v}}{dy^{\prime}_{i}}
equals
-\tfrac{\partial}{\partial Y^{\prime}_{i}}m(z_{v},Y^{\prime}_{i})\big/\tfrac{\partial}{\partial z_{v}}m(z_{v},Y^{\prime}_{i})\big|_{Y^{\prime}_{i}=y^{\prime}_{i}}
where
m(z_{v},Y^{\prime}_{i})\in{\mathbb{F}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[Y^{\prime}_{i}]
is the minimal polynomial of
y^{\prime}_{i}
over
Q_{v}
, and hence
\psi\bigl(\tfrac{dz_{v}}{dy^{\prime}_{i}}\bigr)
is non-zero by the separability of
y^{\prime}_{i}
over
Q_{v}
, and the injectivity of
\psi
on
E_{v,i}
. Now
E_{i}=E^{\prime}_{i}(y_{i})
, respectively
E_{v,i}=E^{\prime}_{v,i}(y_{i})
, and hence
E_{i}\otimes_{E^{\prime}_{i}}K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}=K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})]=K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
, respectively
E_{v,i}\otimes_{E^{\prime}_{v,i}}L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}=L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}[y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})]=L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
with
\bigl(y_{i}-\psi(y_{i})\bigr)^{p^{m}}=y^{\prime}_{i}-\psi(y^{\prime}_{i})
. Since
H_{E}=\bigcup_{i}H_{E_{i}}
, respectively
H_{E_{v}}=\bigcup_{i}H_{E_{v,i}}
, the lemma follows.
∎

Appendix B
Erratum

B.1
First Error

In (
1.13
) and (
1.12
) and Definition
5.21

it is claimed that
v(\omega_{\psi})
and
v_{\psi}(u)
are integers. However, in general they only lie in the rational numbers
{\mathbb{Q}}
, because the valuation
v
is normalized to be an isomorphism
v\colon Q_{v}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}/A_{v}^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}{\mathbb{Z}}
, but the arguments of
v
in both formulas lie in
Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
instead of
Q_{v}
.

This error is harmless, as the integrality of
v_{\psi}(u)
and
v(\omega_{\psi})
is nowhere used.

B.2
Second Error

In Formula (
1.13
) and Definition
4.10

there is an
error in the definition
of
v(\omega_{\psi})
.

As in most of the main text
we fix a finite separable semi-simple
Q
-algebra
E
. That is,
E
is a product of finite separable field extensions of
Q
. We fix a finite place
v
of
Q
and consider the decomposition of the separable
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}:=E\otimes_{Q}Q_{v}=E_{v,1}\times\cdots\times E_{v,s}
into a product of finite field extensions
E_{v,i}
of
Q_{v}
as after Definition
4.1
. We fix a finite Galois extension
K\subset Q^{\rm alg}
of
Q
and we let
L:=K_{v}\subset Q_{v}^{\rm alg}
be the closure of
K
. It is a finite Galois extension of
Q_{v}
. We fix a
\psi\in H_{E}
. The canonical extension
\psi\otimes{\rm id}_{Q_{v}}\colon E_{v}\to L
will be denoted again by
\psi
and factors through the quotient
E_{v,i(\psi)}
of
E_{v}
; see Definition
4.5
.

Let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}
be a local shtuka over
R:={\cal{O}}_{L}
with complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
as in Definition
4.3
. It may arise from a good model
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
of an
A
-motive over
R
as in Example
3.2
. We consider the one-dimensional
L
-vector space

\displaystyle\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\bigl\{\omega\in\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)\colon[a]^{*}\omega=\psi(a)\cdot\omega\kern 5.0pt\forall\;a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\bigr\}

(B.1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{\mbox{\hskip 2.84526pt\raisebox{3.98337pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}\hskip-9.95845pt$\longrightarrow$}}}

\displaystyle\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)\big/([a]^{*}-\psi(a)\colon a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}})\cdot\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)\,,

where the isomorphism comes from Proposition
4.9
using that
E
is separable over
Q
.

In Formula (
1.13
) and Definition
4.10

there is an
error in the definition
of
v(\omega_{\psi})
for
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generators
\omega_{\psi}
of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. Namely, there as reference integral structure on the
L
-vector space
