\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)=\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})\big/\bigl(y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)
the
R
-module

{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\;:=\;\bigl\{\omega\in\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\colon[a]^{*}\omega=\psi(a)\cdot\omega\kern 5.0pt\forall\;a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\bigr\}

was used (which was denoted without the
{\widetilde{~}}
on
{\widetilde{\Koh}}
in (
1.13
) and Definition
4.10
).
Then
v(\omega_{\psi})
was defined to be

v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})\;:=\;v(\tilde{x})\;\in\;{\mathbb{Q}}\,,

(B.2)

where
\tilde{x}\in L^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
satisfies that
\tilde{x}^{-1}\bigl(\omega_{\psi}\;{\rm mod}\;y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)
is an
R
-generator of
{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)
. (To clarify the error we write
v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})
instead of
v(\omega_{\psi})
in this erratum.)

However, in the rest of the main text the
R
-submodule

\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\;:=\;\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\big/([a]^{*}-\psi(a)\colon a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}})\cdot\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\;\subset\;\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)

is used as reference integral structure on
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)
. (See Lemma
B.1
below for why the latter is an inclusion, and how the two integral structures can be compared.) Correspondingly, the following definition for
v(\omega_{\psi})
is used in the main text.

v(\omega_{\psi})\;:=\;v(x)\;\in\;{\mathbb{Q}}\,,

(B.3)

where
x\in L^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
satisfies that
x^{-1}\bigl(\omega_{\psi}\;{\rm mod}\;y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)})\bigr)
is an
R
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)
. Indeed, in
5.12
the generator
\omega_{\psi}^{\circ}:=1
of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)
is used, which might not lie in
{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)
. Afterwards, any other generator
\omega_{\psi}
is compared to the generator
\omega_{\psi}^{\circ}
. This error occurs in Theorems
1.3
and
5.24
and in Corollaries
5.22
and
5.25
. In terms of the valuation
v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})
from (
B.2
), all these theorems and corollaries have to be reformulated as explained below. However, with the definition of
v(\omega_{\psi})
in (
B.3
) above, all these theorems and corollaries are correct.

Note that if
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}={\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{v}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}})
arises from a good model
{\underline{{\cal{M}}}}
of an
A
-motive over
R
as in Example
3.2
, then
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)=\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R):=\sigma^{*}{\cal{M}}\otimes_{A_{R},\,\gamma\otimes{\rm id}_{R}}R
, and hence

\displaystyle{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)\;=\;

\displaystyle{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R)

\displaystyle\;:=\;

\displaystyle\bigl\{\omega\in\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R)\colon[a]^{*}\omega=\psi(a)\cdot\omega\kern 5.0pt\forall\;a\in{\cal{O}}_{E}\bigr\}\,,

\displaystyle\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\;=\;

\displaystyle\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R)

\displaystyle\;:=\;

\displaystyle\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R)\big/([a]^{*}-\psi(a)\colon a\in{\cal{O}}_{E})\cdot\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R)

inside

\displaystyle\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)

\displaystyle\;=\;

\displaystyle\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},L)\;=\;{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R)\otimes_{R}L\;=\;\Koh{}^{\psi}({\underline{{\cal{M}}}},R)\otimes_{R}L\,.

We next show how the two integral structures can be compared.

Lemma B.1
.

The integral structures
{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)
and
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)
are free
R
-modules of rank one and contained in the
L
-vector space
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)
. The natural
R
-morphism

{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\;\lhook\joinrel\longrightarrow\;\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\;\mbox{$\kern 2.0pt\longrightarrow\kern-8.0pt\to\kern 2.0pt$}\;\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)

is injective with cokernel isomorphic to
R/R\cdot\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v}/Q_{v}})
, where
{\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v}/Q_{v}}
is the different of
E_{v}=\prod_{i=1}^{s}E_{v,i}
over
Q_{v}
.

Proof.

The morphism fits into the following diagram

\textstyle{{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\textstyle?}
\textstyle{\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\textstyle\sim}
\textstyle{\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)}

(B.4)

in which the lower isomorphism was described in (
B.1
), the lower triangle is the tensor product of the upper row with
L
, and the injectivity of the right vertical arrow still has to be proved. Note that the argument will not use the specific situation of de Rham cohomology of local shtukas. It will only use the isomorphism (
B.1
) comming from Proposition
4.9
and the freeness of the
R
-module
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
over
{\cal{O}}_{E,v}\otimes_{A_{v}}R
, see below.

The
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}
acts on
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)
through the character
\psi\colon E_{v}\twoheadrightarrow E_{v,i(\psi)}\hookrightarrow L
. By
[
Ser79
, § III.6, Proposition 12]
there exists an element
y\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)}}
such that
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)}}=A_{v}[y]=A_{v}[Y]/(m)
where
m\in A_{v}[Y]
is the minimal polynomial of
y
over
A_{v}
. The image
\gamma(m)
under the map
\gamma\colon A_{v}[Y]\hookrightarrow R[Y]
has
\psi(y)
as a zero and correspondingly factors as

\gamma(m)\;=\;\bigl(Y-\psi(y)\bigr)\cdot g(Y)

for a monic polynomial
g(Y)\in R[Y]
. The derivative
m^{\prime}:=\tfrac{dm}{dY}\in A_{v}[Y]
satisfies

\psi\bigl(m^{\prime}(y)\bigr)\;=\;\gamma(m)^{\prime}\bigl(\psi(y)\bigr)\;=\;g\bigl(\psi(y)\bigr)\,.

(B.5)

Recall that
A_{v,R}
is the
v
-adic completion of
A_{R}
. By Proposition
4.8
we can decompose
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}=\bigoplus_{i=1}^{s}{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i}
into local shtukas
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i}
over
R
with complex multiplication by
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}
. In particular,

\displaystyle{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\bigl\{\omega\in\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i(\psi)},R)\colon[a]^{*}\omega=\psi(a)\cdot\omega\kern 5.0pt\forall\;a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}}\bigr\}\qquad\text{and}

\displaystyle\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i(\psi)},R)\big/([a]^{*}-\psi(a)\colon a\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i}})\cdot\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i(\psi)},R)

can be computed from

\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i(\psi)},R)\;:=\;\sigma^{*}\hat{M}_{i(\psi)}\otimes_{A_{v,R},\;\gamma\otimes{\rm id}_{R}}R

instead of
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
. Moreover, by loc. cit.
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
is free over
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v},R}:={\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}\otimes_{A_{v}}R{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}z{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}={\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}{\widehat{\otimes}}_{{\mathbb{F}}_{v}}R
of rank one, and we may choose a generator of
\hat{M}
. This generator provides an isomorphism

\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{i(\psi)},R)\;\cong\;({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)}}{\widehat{\otimes}}_{{\mathbb{F}}_{q}}R)\underset{A_{v,R},\;\gamma\otimes{\rm id}_{R}}{\otimes}R\;=\;{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)}}\underset{A_{v},\gamma}{\otimes}R\;=\;R[Y]/(\gamma(m))\,.

Since
[a]^{*}-\psi(a)=[a]^{*}-\gamma(a)
for
a\in A_{v}
already annihilates
\Koh^{1}_{\rm dR}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
, this yields the upper vertical isomorphisms in the following diagram

\textstyle{{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\textstyle\cong}
\textstyle{\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\textstyle\cong}
\textstyle{\bigl\{\,f\in{\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)}}\otimes_{A_{v},\gamma}R\colon(y\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(y))\cdot f=0\,\bigr\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{({\cal{O}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)}}\otimes_{A_{v},\gamma}R)\big/(y\otimes 1-1\otimes\psi(y))\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\bigl\{\,f\in R[Y]/(\gamma(m))\colon(Y-\psi(y))\cdot f=0\,\bigr\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{R[Y]/\bigl(\gamma(m),Y-\psi(y)\bigr)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{g(Y)\cdot R[Y]/(\gamma(m))\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{R[Y]/(Y-\psi(y))}

The injectivity of the horizontal maps follows from diagram (
B.4
). The lower left equality holds because
R[Y]
has no
(Y-\psi(y))
-torsion. Next,
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\cong R[Y]/(Y-\psi(y))\cong R
is free, and hence contained in
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},R)\otimes_{R}L=\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{},L)
. Finally, the image of the lower horizontal map is the ideal

R\cdot g\bigl(\psi(y)\bigr)\;=\;R\cdot\psi\bigl(m^{\prime}(y)\bigr)\;=\;R\cdot\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v,i(\psi)}/Q_{v}})\;=\;R\cdot\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v}/Q_{v}})\,;

see
[
Ser79
, § III.4, Proposition 10 and § III.6, Corollary 2]
.
∎

Corollary B.2
.

For an
L
-generator
\omega_{\psi}
of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L)
, the two valuations (
B.2
) and (
B.3
) satisfy

v(\omega_{\psi})-v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})\;=\;v({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E_{v})/Q_{v}})\;=\;v\bigl(\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v}/Q_{v}})\bigr)\;=\;v\bigl(\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E/Q})\bigr)\,.

Proof.

Let
x,\tilde{x}\in L^{\scriptscriptstyle\times}
be elements such that
x^{-1}\omega_{\psi}
is an
R
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
and
\tilde{x}^{-1}\omega_{\psi}
is an
R
-generator of
{\widetilde{\Koh}}{}^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},R)
. Then
x/\tilde{x}
is an
R
-generator of
\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E_{v}/Q_{v}})
by Lemma
B.1
and the corollary follows.
∎

Now let
{\underline{M\!}\,}
be an
A
-motive over a finite Galois extension
K\subset Q^{\rm alg}
of
Q
with complex multiplication by a finite separable semi-simple
Q
-algebra
E
. Assume that
\psi(E)\subset K
for all
\psi\in H_{E}
. Fix a
\psi\in H_{E}
and let
\omega_{\psi}
be a generator of the
K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{\psi}-\psi(y_{\psi}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-module
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{M\!}\,}^{\eta},K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{\psi}-\psi(y_{\psi}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. For every
\eta\in H_{K}
let
{\underline{M\!}\,}^{\eta}
and
\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}\in\Koh^{\eta\psi}({\underline{M\!}\,}^{\eta},K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{\eta\psi}-\eta\psi(y_{\eta\psi}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
be obtained by extension of scalars via
\eta
. With the corollary and the computation

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}v(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})-v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})

\displaystyle\;=\;\textstyle\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}v\bigl(\eta\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E/Q})\bigr)\;=\;\textstyle v\Bigl(\,\prod\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}\eta\psi({\mathfrak{D}}_{E/Q})\Bigr)\;=\;v\bigl(N_{K/Q}({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E)/Q})\bigr)

\displaystyle\;=\;v\Bigl(N_{\psi(E)/Q}\bigl(N_{K/\psi(E)}({\mathfrak{D}}_{\psi(E)/Q})\bigr)\Bigr)\;=\;[K:\psi(E)]\cdot v({\mathfrak{d}}_{\psi({\cal{O}}_{E})/A})

we obtain a reformulation of Theorems
1.3
and
5.24
and Corollaries
5.22
and
5.25
in terms of
v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})
, which is even more analogous to
[
Col93
, Theorem II.1.1(i)]
.

Theorem 1.3’.

Let
\omega_{\psi}
be a generator of the
K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{\psi}-\psi(y_{\psi}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-module
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{M\!}\,},K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{\psi}-\psi(y_{\psi}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. For every
\eta\in H_{K}
let
{\underline{M\!}\,}^{\eta}
and
\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}\in\Koh^{\eta\psi}({\underline{M\!}\,}^{\eta},K{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{\eta\psi}-\eta\psi(y_{\eta\psi}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
be obtained by extension of scalars via
\eta
, and choose an
E
-generator
u_{\eta}\in\Koh_{1,{\rm Betti}}({\underline{M\!}\,}^{\eta},Q)
. Then for every place
v\neq\infty
of
C
we have

\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\#H_{K}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}v({\textstyle\int_{u_{\eta}}\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}})\;=\;Z_{v}(a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a^{0}_{E,\psi,\Phi})+\tfrac{1}{\#H_{K}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{K}}\bigl(v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})+v_{\eta\psi}(u_{\eta})\bigr)\,.\qed

Corollary 5.22’.

Let
\varphi,\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
with
i(\varphi)=i(\psi)=:i
and assume that
E_{v,i}
is separable over
Q_{v}
. Let
u\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},Q_{v})
be a generator as
E_{v}
-module and let
\omega_{\psi}
be an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}_{E_{v},\varphi},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i}-\psi(y_{i}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. Then
\int_{u}\omega_{\psi}:=u\otimes{\rm id}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}}\bigl(h^{-1}_{v,{\rm dR}}(\omega_{\psi})\bigr)
has valuation

v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\varphi})+v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})+v_{\psi}(u)\,,

where
v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})
and
v_{\psi}(u)
were defined in (
B.2
) and Definition
5.21
, respectively.
∎

Theorem 5.24’.

Let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}
be a local shtuka over
R
with complex multiplication by the ring of integers
{\cal{O}}_{E_{v}}
in a commutative, semi-simple, separable
Q_{v}
-algebra
E_{v}
with local CM-type
\Phi
, and assume that
\psi(E_{v})\subset L
for all
\psi\in H_{E_{v}}
and that
L
is separable over
Q_{v}
. Let
u\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},Q_{v})
be an
E_{v}
-generator and let
\omega_{\psi}
be an
L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}}
-generator of
\Koh^{\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
. Then the period
\int_{u}\omega_{\psi}:=u\otimes{\rm id}_{{\mathbb{C}}_{v}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm(\hskip-1.19579pt(}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm(\hskip-1.0208pt(}}}z-\zeta{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm)\hskip-1.19579pt)}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm)\hskip-1.0208pt)}}}}\bigl(h^{-1}_{v,{\rm dR}}(\omega_{\psi})\bigr)
has valuation

v\bigl({\textstyle\int_{u}}\omega_{\psi}\bigr)\;=\;Z_{v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi})+v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})+v_{\psi}(u)\,,

where
v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi})
and
v_{\psi}(u)
were defined in (
B.2
) and Definition
5.21
, respectively.
∎

Corollary 5.25’.

Keep the situation of Theorem 5.24’. For every
\eta\in H_{L}
note that
i(\eta\psi)=i(\psi)
, let
{\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta}
and
\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}\in\Koh^{\eta\psi}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta},L{\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\rm[\hskip-1.49994pt[}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm[\hskip-1.19579pt[}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm[\hskip-1.0208pt[}}}y_{i(\psi)}-\eta\psi(y_{i(\psi)}){\mathchoice{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\rm]\hskip-1.49994pt]}}{\mbox{\scriptsize\rm]\hskip-1.19579pt]}}{\mbox{\tiny\rm]\hskip-1.0208pt]}}})
be obtained by extension of scalars via
\eta
, and choose an
E_{v}
-generator
u_{\eta}\in\Koh_{1,v}({\underline{\hat{M}\!}\,}{}^{\eta},Q_{v})
. Then

\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\#H_{L}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{L}}v({\textstyle\int_{u_{\eta}}\omega_{\psi}^{\eta}})\;=\;Z_{v}(a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi},1)-\mu_{{\rm Art},v}(a^{0}_{E_{v},\psi,\Phi})+\tfrac{1}{\#H_{L}}\sum\limits_{\eta\in H_{L}}\bigl(v^{\sim}(\omega_{\psi}^{\eta})+v_{\eta\psi}(u_{\eta})\bigr)\,.\qed
